<p class='hn-title'>(1) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://mitchellh.com/writing/ghostty-leaving-github' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Ghostty is leaving GitHub</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>Ghostty 将离开 GitHub</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47939579' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 3448</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47939579' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 1029</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--green' data-tag='Open Source'>Open Source</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--sky' data-tag='Career'>Career</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--indigo' data-tag='Programming'>Programming</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Ghostty is leaving GitHub, signaling concerns about platform bind and the viability of OSS workflows outside centralized hosting. The move prompts reflection on dependencies, portability, and how maintainers plan long-term collaboration without GitHub’s ecosystem.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>Ghostty 将离开 GitHub，凸显了在中心化托管之外维持开源工作流的挑战与风险。此举引发对依赖关系、可移植性以及维护者在无 GitHub 生态的情况下如何长期协作的思考。</p>
</div>

<p class='hn-title'>(2) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://wheelfront.com/this-alberta-startup-sells-no-tech-tractors-for-half-price/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Alberta startup sells no-tech tractors for half price</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>阿尔伯塔初创以半价销售无科技拖拉机</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47865868' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 2250</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47865868' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 761</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--amber' data-tag='Startups'>Startups</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--amber' data-tag='Business'>Business</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>An Alberta startup is selling traditional, no-tech tractors at half the price of modern models, targeting farmers who value simplicity and repairability. By removing electronics, they cut upfront costs and maintenance headaches, challenging mainstream ag-tech pricing and support models.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>一家阿尔伯塔初创企业以半价销售无科技的拖拉机，瞄准看重简单与易修的农民。去除电子系统后，制造与维护成本下降，这一做法可能影响主流农业科技的定价和售后模式。</p>
</div>
</div>
</div>

<div class='hn-body'>

<p class='hn-title'>(3) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://piechowski.io/post/git-commands-before-reading-code/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Git commands I run before reading any code</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>在读代码前我会执行的 Git 命令</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47687273' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 2244</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47687273' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 477</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--indigo' data-tag='Programming'>Programming</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Before diving into a codebase, I run a concise set of Git commands to map the repo’s history, structure, and hotspots. The routine surfaces remotes, recent commits, file changes, and likely entry points, helping you jump straight to meaningful parts.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>在进入代码库前，作者先执行一小组 Git 命令来快速梳理历史、结构与关键区域。这个流程能快速显示远端、最近提交、改动文件以及潜在入口点，帮助开发者直接进入重点代码。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://piechowski.io/post/git-commands-before-reading-code/cover_hu3f66e25b7571f7e32d40f355f31a2ca9_56928_1200x630_resize_box_2.png' data-full='https://piechowski.io/post/git-commands-before-reading-code/cover_hu3f66e25b7571f7e32d40f355f31a2ca9_56928_1200x630_resize_box_2.png' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>

<p class='hn-title'>(4) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://www.apple.com/newsroom/2026/04/tim-cook-to-become-apple-executive-chairman-john-ternus-to-become-apple-ceo/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>John Ternus to become Apple CEO</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>约翰·特诺斯将任苹果公司 CEO</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47840219' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 2157</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47840219' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 1301</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--amber' data-tag='Business'>Business</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--slate' data-tag='Hardware'>Hardware</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Apple has announced a leadership transition with Tim Cook moving to executive chairman and John Ternus named CEO, signaling a planned succession. Ternus, a veteran hardware and product operations leader, would steer Apple’s product roadmap and hardware/software integration in the next era. The move underscores Apple’s emphasis on continuity and a hardware-centric strategy.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>苹果宣布高层交接，蒂姆·库克将出任执行董事长，约翰·特诺斯接任 CEO。特诺斯长期担任硬件/产品运营负责人，接任后将引领苹果的产品路线与硬件/软件协同。此举突显苹果在保持连续性和以硬件驱动战略方面的长期规划。</p>

<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Sam Altman&#x27;s influence over AI policy and development raises questions about the balance of power and accountability in tech leadership. <p class='hn-title'>(9) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://www.stepsecurity.io/blog/axios-compromised-on-npm-malicious-versions-drop-remote-access-trojan' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Axios compromised on NPM – Malicious versions drop remote access trojan</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>Axios 在 NPM 上被劫持：恶意版本投放远程访问木马</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47582220' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 1904</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47582220' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 785</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--red' data-tag='Security'>Security</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--indigo' data-tag='Programming'>Programming</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Malicious Axios releases popped up on npm, embedding a remote access Trojan in certain versions. The attack highlights the ongoing supply-chain risk for widely used libraries, and operators should audit dependencies, pin safe versions, and monitor for unusual network activity after updates.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>npm 上出现恶意的 Axios 版本，包含可对受影响环境进行远程访问的木马。此类供应链攻击再次暴露了对广泛使用库的依赖风险，建议锁定版本、进行依赖审计，并在更新后监测异常网络活动。</p>

</div>
</div>

<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>我把 Mac OS X 移植到了任天堂 Wii</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47691730' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 1877</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47691730' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 317</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--indigo' data-tag='Programming'>Programming</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--slate' data-tag='Hardware'>Hardware</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--purple' data-tag='Gaming'>Gaming</span></div>

<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>A personal experiment porting Mac OS X to the Nintendo Wii, outlining the core challenges of cross-hardware porting and what it reveals about software portability. The write-up highlights bootstrapping, driver gaps, and the kinds of trade-offs required when attempting to run a modern OS on a legacy console.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>作者进行了将 Mac OS X 移植到任天堂 Wii 的个人尝试，概述了跨硬件移植的核心挑战以及软件可移植性在极端约束下的意义。文章聚焦引导启动、驱动缺口，以及在旧世代主机上运行现代操作系统所需的取舍。</p>

<p class='hn-title'>(11) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://browsergate.eu/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>LinkedIn is searching your browser extensions</a></p>

<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47613981' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 1866</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47613981' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 755</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--red' data-tag='Privacy'>Privacy</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--violet' data-tag='Data'>Data</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--cyan' data-tag='Web'>Web</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>A report suggests LinkedIn collects data from installed browser extensions, raising privacy and data-use questions for a platform that handles professional identity and recruitment. The practice highlights tensions between data collection for service improvement and user privacy.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>有报道指 LinkedIn 会收集已安装的浏览器扩展数据，引发关于隐私和数据使用的质疑，尤其是在处理专业身份与招聘的场景中。这一做法凸显了服务改进与用户隐私之间的紧张关系。</p>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-47616361' data-tags='AI,Open Source,Hardware'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<button class='hn-digest-toggle' type='button' aria-expanded='false' title='Show summary and image' aria-label='Show summary and image'><span aria-hidden='true'>⌄</span></button>
<p class='hn-title'>(12) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://deepmind.google/models/gemma/gemma-4/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Google releases Gemma 4 open models</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>谷歌发布 Gemma 4 开源模型</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47616361' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 1759</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47616361' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 462</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--blue' data-tag='AI'>AI</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--green' data-tag='Open Source'>Open Source</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--slate' data-tag='Hardware'>Hardware</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Google releases Gemma 4 open models, expanding the Gemma family with a newer generation that can be run by researchers and developers on their own hardware. This move underscores Google&#x27;s push toward open, auditable AI and could accelerate experimentation outside managed cloud environments.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>谷歌发布 Gemma 4 开源模型，提供新一代可供研究者和开发者在本地硬件上运行的能力。这一举动凸显谷歌向开放、可审计的 AI 生态迈进，可能加速在自有环境中的研究与实验。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://lh3.googleusercontent.com/XtdGQ6B0XzfV1gByFCtGbxLthooBSyg6QZWJn4VuM4SpDduwdYqOPFWpxyMIv875LTG1zUt0Xm34B29PfjACwd6m8fLiQpFLkZnjR5bVmJnCDANx6g=w1200-h630-n-nu-rw' data-full='https://lh3.googleusercontent.com/XtdGQ6B0XzfV1gByFCtGbxLthooBSyg6QZWJn4VuM4SpDduwdYqOPFWpxyMIv875LTG1zUt0Xm34B29PfjACwd6m8fLiQpFLkZnjR5bVmJnCDANx6g=w1200-h630-n-nu-rw' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>

<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-47782570' data-tags='Privacy,Politics'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<button class='hn-digest-toggle' type='button' aria-expanded='false' title='Show summary and image' aria-label='Show summary and image'><span aria-hidden='true'>⌄</span></button>
<p class='hn-title'>(13) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://www.eff.org/deeplinks/2026/04/google-broke-its-promise-me-now-ice-has-my-data' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Google broke its promise to me – now ICE has my data</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>谷歌违背承诺，现在 ICE 已掌握我的数据</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47782570' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 1681</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47782570' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 747</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--red' data-tag='Privacy'>Privacy</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--orange' data-tag='Politics'>Politics</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Google&#x27;s data sharing with ICE, despite promises, highlights how government access can negate user privacy. The case underscores the tension between platform commitments and law enforcement data demands, and the need for stronger privacy controls.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>谷歌在承诺后将部分用户数据提供给 ICE，暴露了政府访问如何侵蚀用户隐私的风险。此事凸显平台承诺与执法需求之间的矛盾，强调需要更强的隐私控制与透明度。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://www.eff.org/files/banner_library/google-spy-eye.png' data-full='https://www.eff.org/files/banner_library/google-spy-eye.png' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-47935853' data-tags='Privacy'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<button class='hn-digest-toggle' type='button' aria-expanded='false' title='Show summary and image' aria-label='Show summary and image'><span aria-hidden='true'>⌄</span></button>
<p class='hn-title'>(14) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://keepandroidopen.org/en/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Your phone is about to stop being yours</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>手机快要不再属于你</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47935853' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 1659</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47935853' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 866</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--red' data-tag='Privacy'>Privacy</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>A campaign argues that smartphones are slipping from users&#x27; ownership due to platform policies and data monopolies. It advocates for user-sovereignty, open devices, and alternative business models.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>一场运动呼吁重新夺回对智能手机的控制权，警惕平台政策与数据垄断带来的用户所有权流失，并倡导用户主权、开源设备与替代商业模式。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://keepandroidopen.org/img/lockdowns/android_lockdown_banner1.png' data-full='https://keepandroidopen.org/img/lockdowns/android_lockdown_banner1.png' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>

<div class='hn-body'>
<button class='hn-digest-toggle' type='button' aria-expanded='false' title='Show summary and image' aria-label='Show summary and image'><span aria-hidden='true'>⌄</span></button>
<p class='hn-title'>(15) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://openai.com/index/introducing-gpt-5-5/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>GPT-5.5</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>GPT-5.5 发布</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47879092' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 1556</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47879092' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 1033</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--blue' data-tag='AI'>AI</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>OpenAI unveils GPT-5.5, the latest mid-cycle upgrade in the GPT-5 family. It emphasizes faster inference, safer defaults, and enhanced developer controls for production deployments, signaling another step in the rapid evolution of foundation models.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>OpenAI 发布了 GPT-5.5，作为 GPT-5 线的中期升级。新版本强调更快的推理与更安全的默认设置，并为开发者提供更丰富的部署控制，再次推动大型语言模型的迭代速度。</p>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-47679121' data-tags='Security,AI'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<button class='hn-digest-toggle' type='button' aria-expanded='false' title='Show summary and image' aria-label='Show summary and image'><span aria-hidden='true'>⌄</span></button>
<p class='hn-title'>(16) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://www.anthropic.com/glasswing' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Project Glasswing: Securing critical software for the AI era</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>玻璃翼计划：为AI时代的关键软件提供安全防护</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47679121' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 1502</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47679121' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 810</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--red' data-tag='Security'>Security</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--blue' data-tag='AI'>AI</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Anthropic&#x27;s Project Glasswing proposes hardened security controls for critical software in AI-enabled environments, addressing supply-chain integrity, code provenance, and runtime protections. The initiative signals a shift toward AI-era security by treating software as an active, risk-prone surface rather than a static asset.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>Anthropic 的 Glasswing 项目提出在 AI 时代加强关键软件的安全控制，覆盖供应链完整性、代码来源可追溯性和运行时防护。该举措标志着安全从静态资产转向对AI驱动环境中高风险表面的主动防护。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://cdn.sanity.io/images/4zrzovbb/website/65641e7846f10255c4a3415a10bbf5793ae87b13-1200x630.jpg' data-full='https://cdn.sanity.io/images/4zrzovbb/website/65641e7846f10255c4a3415a10bbf5793ae87b13-1200x630.jpg' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>

<p class='hn-title'>(17) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://frame.work/laptop13pro' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Framework Laptop 13 Pro</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>Framework Laptop 13 Pro：模块化可维修笔记本</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47852177' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 1443</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47852177' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 749</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--slate' data-tag='Hardware'>Hardware</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--green' data-tag='Open Source'>Open Source</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Framework Laptop 13 Pro continues the modular, repair-friendly approach with upgraded internals and options. The release underscores the viability of upgradable hardware versus disposable laptops. It matters for developers and hardware enthusiasts who value sustainability and customization.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>Framework Laptop 13 Pro 延续了模块化、便于维修的设计，并升级了内部硬件选项。此次发布强调可升级硬件相对于一次性笔记本的可行性，对重视可持续性与自定义性的开发者与硬件爱好者尤为重要。</p>
</div>

<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-47834195' data-tags='Legal,Business'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<button class='hn-digest-toggle' type='button' aria-expanded='false' title='Show summary and image' aria-label='Show summary and image'><span aria-hidden='true'>⌄</span></button>
<p class='hn-title'>(18) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://www.theolivepress.es/spain-news/2026/04/20/eu-to-force-replaceable-batteries-in-phones-and-tablets-from-2027/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>All phones sold in the EU to have replaceable batteries from 2027</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>到2027年，欧盟销售的所有手机将配备可更换电池</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47834195' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 1429</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47834195' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 1246</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--orange' data-tag='Legal'>Legal</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--amber' data-tag='Business'>Business</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>The EU will require replaceable batteries in all phones starting 2027, aiming to extend device lifespans and reduce e-waste. The rule could force redesigns, disrupt supply chains, and influence consumer costs.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>欧盟将于2027年要求所有手机具备可更换电池，旨在延长设备寿命并减少电子垃圾。此举可能迫使厂商重新设计，影响供应链并提高一些消费者成本。</p>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-47706268' data-tags='Privacy,Politics'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<button class='hn-digest-toggle' type='button' aria-expanded='false' title='Show summary and image' aria-label='Show summary and image'><span aria-hidden='true'>⌄</span></button>
<p class='hn-title'>(19) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://www.eff.org/deeplinks/2026/04/eff-leaving-x' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>EFF is leaving X</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>EFF 将离开 X</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47706268' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 1409</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47706268' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 1277</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--red' data-tag='Privacy'>Privacy</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--orange' data-tag='Politics'>Politics</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>EFF announces departure from X, citing concerns over platform policies, data practices, and civil liberties. This move signals growing tensions between advocacy groups and major social platforms, and may influence developers and researchers who rely on the platform for distribution or outreach.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>电子前哨基金会宣布退出 X，表示对平台政策、数据使用等问题存在关切。这一举动凸显了公民社会组织与大型社交平台之间的日益紧张关系，并可能影响依赖该平台进行传播与研究的开发者与研究者。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://www.eff.org/files/banner_library/Twitter-censored.png' data-full='https://www.eff.org/files/banner_library/Twitter-censored.png' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>

<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Copy Fail catalogs misprints, copy errors, and UX writing flubs from tech projects, highlighting how small wording mistakes can derail user understanding. It’s a reminder for developers to invest in clear, consistent copy.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>Copy Fail 汇集科技项目中的印刷与文案错误，展示用词不当如何扰乱用户理解。提醒开发者重视清晰、连贯的文案。</p>

<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>A playful hardware tweak: the author files the corners of their MacBooks to smooth edges, sharing steps, tools, and cautions about warranty and durability. It doubles as a meditation on DIY customization vs. risk of damaging laptops.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>作者动手把 MacBook 的边角削圆，给出具体步骤与所用工具，并讨论此改造对保修与耐用性的影响。全文从极客自我改造的角度，兼具风险评估的思考。</p>

<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-47586778' data-tags='AI,Security'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<button class='hn-digest-toggle' type='button' aria-expanded='false' title='Show summary and image' aria-label='Show summary and image'><span aria-hidden='true'>⌄</span></button>
<p class='hn-title'>(22) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://alex000kim.com/posts/2026-03-31-claude-code-source-leak/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>The Claude Code Source Leak: fake tools, frustration regexes, undercover mode</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>Claude 代码源泄露：假工具、挫败感正则、潜伏模式</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47586778' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 1352</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47586778' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 558</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--blue' data-tag='AI'>AI</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--red' data-tag='Security'>Security</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>An analysis of a Claude code-source leak reveals decoy tools and frustrating regex tests, with mentions of an undercover mode. The incident highlights the fragility of model provenance and the risk of leaked tooling being manipulated to mislead researchers. The takeaway is a call for stronger provenance controls and safer, auditable tooling around AI code-generation.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>对 Claude 代码源泄露事件的分析显示存在伪工具与令人挫败的正则测试，并提及潜伏模式。此事凸显了模型来源的脆弱性，以及泄露工具被操纵以误导研究者的风险。核心启示是需要加强来源追溯与对 AI 代码生成工具的安全、可审计设计。</p>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-47697870' data-tags='Security'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<button class='hn-digest-toggle' type='button' aria-expanded='false' title='Show summary and image' aria-label='Show summary and image'><span aria-hidden='true'>⌄</span></button>
<p class='hn-title'>(23) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://obdev.at/products/littlesnitch-linux/index.html' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>LittleSnitch for Linux</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>Linux 版 Little Snitch</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47697870' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 1328</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47697870' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 439</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--red' data-tag='Security'>Security</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>A Linux port of the macOS outbound firewall LittleSnitch arrives, enabling per-app network monitoring and rule-based controls for Linux users. This signals growing demand for fine-grained, user-friendly network policies on desktop Linux and could influence future firewall tooling and UX.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>Linux 版 Little Snitch 为桌面 Linux 增添按应用层级进行出站网络控制的能力，用户可对应用的网络请求逐个批准或阻止。这类细分的网络策略工具日益成为需求，可能推动未来防火墙工具的用户体验与实现方向。</p>

</div>

<p class='hn-title'>(24) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://github.com/anthropics/claude-code/issues/42796' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Issue: Claude Code is unusable for complex engineering tasks with Feb updates</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>Claude Code 在二月更新后难以处理复杂工程任务</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47660925' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 1316</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47660925' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 727</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--blue' data-tag='AI'>AI</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--indigo' data-tag='Programming'>Programming</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>A user reports Claude Code is unusable for complex engineering tasks after February updates, citing issues with multi-file projects, debugging, and reliability. The note highlights current limits of AI coding assistants for serious production work and the need for ongoing improvements.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>有用户反映在二月更新后，Claude Code 难以处理复杂工程任务，涉及多文件项目、调试与可靠性等问题。该评测强调了 AI 编码助手在正式生产场景中的局限性，以及持续改进的必要性。</p>

<p class='hn-title'>(25) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://www.cbsnews.com/live-updates/artemis-ii-splashdown-return/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Artemis II safely splashes down</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>阿耳忒弥斯II号安全落水回归</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47725583' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 1256</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47725583' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 421</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--teal' data-tag='Space'>Space</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>NASA&#x27;s Artemis II mission completed a safe splashdown, marking a milestone in crewed lunar exploration. The return validates deep-space readiness and endurance in human spaceflight, setting the stage for subsequent lunar missions and partnerships with industry.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>阿耳忒弥斯II号完成安全落水，标志着载人月球探测的重要里程碑。此次归来验证了深空任务的可行性与人类航天的耐久性，并为后续任务及产业合作奠定基础。</p>

<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-47732020' data-tags='AI,Security'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<button class='hn-digest-toggle' type='button' aria-expanded='false' title='Show summary and image' aria-label='Show summary and image'><span aria-hidden='true'>⌄</span></button>
<p class='hn-title'>(26) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://aisle.com/blog/ai-cybersecurity-after-mythos-the-jagged-frontier' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Small models also found the vulnerabilities that Mythos found</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>小模型也发现了 Mythos 发现的漏洞</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47732020' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 1254</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47732020' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 330</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--blue' data-tag='AI'>AI</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--red' data-tag='Security'>Security</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>New findings show small models exhibit the same vulnerabilities Mythos uncovered in larger models, underscoring that model-size alone doesn&#x27;t grant security. This broadens risk assessment to include a wider range of models and prompts tighter evaluation and defense strategies.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>研究表明，即使是小型模型也存在 Mythos 所揭示的漏洞，说明漏洞并非只有大模型才有。这使安全评估需覆盖更广的模型范围，并推动更严格的测试与防护措施。</p>

<div class='hn-body'>
<button class='hn-digest-toggle' type='button' aria-expanded='false' title='Show summary and image' aria-label='Show summary and image'><span aria-hidden='true'>⌄</span></button>
<p class='hn-title'>(28) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://github.com/anthropics/claude-code/issues/53262' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>HERMES.md in commit messages causes requests to route to extra usage billing</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>提交信息中的 HERMES.md 会将请求路由至额外的使用量计费</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47952722' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 1236</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47952722' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 517</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--indigo' data-tag='Programming'>Programming</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--amber' data-tag='Finance'>Finance</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>A misconfigured billing rule triggers extra usage charges whenever a commit message contains the string HERMES.md. The quirk demonstrates how tiny pieces of metadata in version control can unexpectedly affect costs, and suggests teams sanitize commit messages or adjust policy to avoid surprise bills.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>提交信息中包含 HERMES.md 会把请求路由到额外的使用量计费，这暴露了版本控制元数据对成本的潜在影响。为避免意外账单，团队应对提交信息进行清洗并审查计费策略。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://opengraph.githubassets.com/f921c1ed5c234ae8e7853d8a30cba83d2c37cb3e6327bc8389dbb6075de57818/anthropics/claude-code/issues/53262' data-full='https://opengraph.githubassets.com/f921c1ed5c234ae8e7853d8a30cba83d2c37cb3e6327bc8389dbb6075de57818/anthropics/claude-code/issues/53262' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>

<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>一位前 Azure Core 工程师揭示削弱用户信任的决策，涉及功能弃用、更新不透明与治理摩擦等问题，令开发者和管理员感到挫败。该分析将 Azure 的演变视为云信任与产品治理的案例研究。</p>

<p class='hn-title'>(34) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://anchor.host/someone-bought-30-wordpress-plugins-and-planted-a-backdoor-in-all-of-them/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Someone bought 30 WordPress plugins and planted a backdoor in all of them</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>有人买下 30 个 WordPress 插件并在全部插件中植入后门</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47755629' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 1168</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47755629' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 326</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--red' data-tag='Security'>Security</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>A security researcher demonstrates the depth of supply-chain risk in plugin ecosystems by buying 30 WordPress plugins and inserting a backdoor into each one. The stunt highlights how third-party extensions can compromise many sites if integrity checks and monitoring are lax.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>安全研究者展示了通过购买 30 个 WordPress 插件并在每一个插件中植入后门，能够大范围入侵网站。这暴露了第三方插件的供应链和信任机制漏洞，强调加强插件审计、完整性校验和运行时监控的重要性。</p>

<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>西方忘记了如何制造，现在又忘记了如何编码</p>

<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Western economies have lost touch with making things, and now their coding skills are at risk as software becomes more opaque. The coverage typically includes countdown progress, weather delays, crew status, and mission objectives for Orion’s first crewed lunar loop since Apollo. This day marks a milestone for the Artemis program and human return to the Moon, with implications for future crewed missions and commercial partnerships.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>在阿尔忒弥斯 II 发射日，NASA 实时更新将持续揭示倒计时进展、天气状况、乘员情况及猎户座任务目标等要点。这标志着阿尔忒弥斯计划的一次重要里程碑，并对未来载人登陆月球与商业伙伴关系产生深远影响。</p>

<p class='hn-title'>(45) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://www.bbc.com/news/articles/ce8jzr423p9o' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Artemis II crew take “spectacular” image of Earth</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>阿尔忒弥斯二号任务组拍出壮观的地球照片</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47631118' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 1047</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47631118' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 358</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--teal' data-tag='Space'>Space</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>The Artemis II crew captured a spectacular image of Earth from near-space, showcasing our planet in a breathtaking vantage point during the mission phase. The shot underscores NASA&#x27;s focus on crewed lunar exploration and the growing role of high-quality space photography in public engagement. It also highlights the technical readiness required for long-duration deep-space missions.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>阿尔忒弥斯二号任务组在近空间阶段拍摄出地球的壮观影像，从独特视角展现了这颗行星的美丽与脆弱。这张照片体现了 NASA 对载人月球探索的持续推进，以及高质量太空影像在公众参与中的作用，同时也凸显了支撑长期深空任务的技术准备。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://ichef.bbci.co.uk/news/1024/branded_news/383b/live/6c4ec320-2f7b-11f1-934f-036468834728.jpg' data-full='https://ichef.bbci.co.uk/news/1024/branded_news/383b/live/6c4ec320-2f7b-11f1-934f-036468834728.jpg' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>

<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>OpenAI unveils ChatGPT Images 2.0, expanding multimodal capabilities by enabling richer image generation and editing inside chats. It promises higher fidelity visuals, more robust prompting controls, and faster rendering, making it easier for creators and developers to prototype visual AI tools directly in conversation.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>OpenAI 发布 ChatGPT 图像 2.0，扩展多模态能力，在对话中实现更丰富的图片生成与编辑。该更新带来更高保真度的图像、更强的提示控制和更快的渲染速度，为创作者和开发者在对话中原生原型化视觉 AI 工具提供新途径。</p>

<div class='hn-body'>

<p class='hn-title'>(48) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://openai.com/index/codex-for-almost-everything/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Codex for almost everything</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>几乎适用于一切场景的 Codex</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47796469' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 990</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47796469' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 540</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--blue' data-tag='AI'>AI</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--indigo' data-tag='Programming'>Programming</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>OpenAI expands Codex beyond code generation into a broader set of tasks, positioning it as a universal AI assistant for developers and operators. The move signals growing demand for single-model copilots that can handle coding, data tasks, automation, and workflow integration, reducing context-switching.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>OpenAI 将 Codex 的应用扩展到广泛任务，将其定位为开发者与运维的通用 AI 助手。此举反映出市场对单一模型兼顾编码、数据处理、自动化与工作流整合的需求日增，减少上下文切换。</p>
</div>
</div>
</div>

<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>An experimental Linux compatibility layer aims to run Linux userland on Windows 9x-era systems. It demonstrates the ongoing appeal of cross-OS compatibility, even in retro environments, and offers insight into how modern tooling could be adapted for older platforms.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>一个实验性 Linux 兼容层尝试在 Windows 95/98 等旧系统上运行 Linux 用户空间。它体现了跨操作系统兼容性的持续吸引力，并为将来在老平台上应用现代工具提供了洞见。</p>

<div class='hn-body'>

<p class='hn-title'>(50) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://www.bloomberg.com/news/articles/2026-04-27/microsoft-to-stop-sharing-revenue-with-main-ai-partner-openai' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Microsoft and OpenAI end their exclusive and revenue-sharing deal</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>微软与 OpenAI 终止独家及分成协议</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47921248' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 969</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47921248' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 839</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--blue' data-tag='AI'>AI</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--amber' data-tag='Business'>Business</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Microsoft and OpenAI are ending their exclusive, revenue-sharing deal. The move loosens a decade-long alignment that helped accelerate AI adoption in Azure and Copilot, while inviting new partnerships for OpenAI and forcing Microsoft to recalibrate its cloud-AI bets. The change reframes the competitive landscape for AI platforms and developer access.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>微软与 OpenAI 终止了此前的独家及分成安排，打破两家长期的深度绑定。此举让 OpenAI 能与更多伙伴合作，微软也需重新布局云端 AI 战略。此举可能重塑 AI 平台生态的竞争格局与开发者接入格局。</p>
</div>
</div>
</div>

<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>1D Chess challenges players with a single-line board, forcing rethinking of piece mobility and strategy. It’s a compact exploration of how constraints alter classical chess dynamics and makes for a playful, AI-friendly coding or game-design exercise.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>一维棋盘上的棋局迫使玩家重新思考移动规则与策略，极简化的设定揭示了约束如何改变经典棋局的动力学。也是一个有趣的 AI 研究/游戏设计练习，便于实现与教学。</p>

<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47863217' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 963</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47863217' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 437</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--blue' data-tag='AI'>AI</span></div>

<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Qwen3.6-27B demonstrates flagship-level coding capabilities in a compact 27-billion-parameter model, suggesting strong performance on programming tasks without massive scale. The result highlights progress in efficient, accessible model sizes for developers.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>Qwen3.6-27B 在仅27亿参数的紧凑模型上展现旗舰级编码能力，表明在编程任务上已经具备出色性能，而无需大规模模型。此成果凸显了在高效、易获得的模型规模上实现进步。</p>

<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Online age verification is framed as a policy-critical tool for safeguarding minors online, but it raises privacy and accessibility concerns. The piece argues this is a central battleground for technology design and regulation.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>在线年龄验证被视为保护未成年人的关键工具，但也引发隐私与可访问性担忧。文章强调这是技术设计与监管的核心争论点。</p>

<div class='hn-body'>
<button class='hn-digest-toggle' type='button' aria-expanded='false' title='Show summary and image' aria-label='Show summary and image'><span aria-hidden='true'>⌄</span></button>
<p class='hn-title'>(56) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://www.nasa.gov/gallery/lunar-flyby/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Lunar Flyby</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>月球飞掠</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47676509' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 943</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47676509' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 243</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--teal' data-tag='Space'>Space</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>NASA recently showcased imagery and data from a lunar flyby, illustrating how spacecraft skim past the Moon to study gravity assists, lighting, and surface features. Such flybys refine trajectory design for future missions and provide rich science visuals for public outreach. They also help calibrate navigation systems used across deep-space operations.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>NASA 展示来自月球近距飞掠的影像与数据，展示航天器在靠近月球时进行引力辅助、照明与表面特征研究的能力。此类飞掠有助于优化未来任务的轨道设计并为公众科普提供直观的科学画面。它们还用于校准深空任务的导航系统。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://www.nasa.gov/wp-content/uploads/2026/04/art002e009288orig.jpg' data-full='https://www.nasa.gov/wp-content/uploads/2026/04/art002e009288orig.jpg' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-47878633' data-tags='Politics,Privacy,Data'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<button class='hn-digest-toggle' type='button' aria-expanded='false' title='Show summary and image' aria-label='Show summary and image'><span aria-hidden='true'>⌄</span></button>
<p class='hn-title'>(57) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://www.wired.com/story/palantir-employees-are-starting-to-wonder-if-theyre-the-bad-guys/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Palantir employees are starting to wonder if they&#x27;re the bad guys</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>Palantir 员工开始质疑自己是否在做坏事</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47878633' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 933</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47878633' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 694</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--orange' data-tag='Politics'>Politics</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--red' data-tag='Privacy'>Privacy</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--violet' data-tag='Data'>Data</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Palantir employees are starting to question whether their work contributes to harmful surveillance and wrongdoing. As Palantir expands government-facing analytics, staff wrestle with ethical concerns about surveillance, data ethics, and public accountability.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>Palantir 员工在公司日益扩张的政府数据分析工作中，开始质疑这是否在助长监控与潜在的滥用。伦理、数据治理与公共问责成为内部讨论的焦点。</p>

<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>玩家在游戏中设计并组装显卡，探索架构选择如何影响性能。该项目展示了将硬件工程概念以游戏化方式呈现的潜力，能激发学习和动手兴趣。</p>

<div class='hn-body'>

<p class='hn-title'>(60) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://www.0xsid.com/blog/wont-download-your-app' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>I won&#x27;t download your app. The web version is a-ok</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>我不想下载你的应用，网页版本就够用</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47661439' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 918</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47661439' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 551</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--cyan' data-tag='Web'>Web</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>A web-first stance: the author chooses not to install a native app and relies on the web version instead. Web apps can be quicker to update and lighter on device storage, reducing platform lock-in, though they may trade off certain features like push notifications and offline access.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>作者坚持网页优先，不安装原生应用，而选择网页版本。网页应用更新更快、占用存储更少，降低了对特定平台的绑定，但在推送通知和离线功能等方面存在权衡。</p>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-47866697' data-tags='Privacy,Security,Web'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<button class='hn-digest-toggle' type='button' aria-expanded='false' title='Show summary and image' aria-label='Show summary and image'><span aria-hidden='true'>⌄</span></button>
<p class='hn-title'>(61) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://fingerprint.com/blog/firefox-tor-indexeddb-privacy-vulnerability/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>We found a stable Firefox identifier linking all your private Tor identities</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>发现 Firefox 的稳定标识符可连接你所有的 Tor 身份</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47866697' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 911</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47866697' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 284</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--red' data-tag='Privacy'>Privacy</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--red' data-tag='Security'>Security</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--cyan' data-tag='Web'>Web</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Researchers found a persistent Firefox identifier can link a user&#x27;s private Tor identities across sessions, undermining anonymity. The finding underscores how browser storage features can erode privacy in privacy-focused tooling and calls for mitigations.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>研究人员发现 Firefox 的持久标识符可在会话之间关联用户的 Tor 身份，削弱匿名性。这一发现揭示了浏览器存储机制对隐私工具构成的潜在威胁，并需要相应缓解措施。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://fingerprint.com/static/893bf56cdf5a22443c3b3ace0bb862df/blog_firefox_vulnerability.jpg' data-full='https://fingerprint.com/static/893bf56cdf5a22443c3b3ace0bb862df/blog_firefox_vulnerability.jpg' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-47587935' data-tags='Business'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<button class='hn-digest-toggle' type='button' aria-expanded='false' title='Show summary and image' aria-label='Show summary and image'><span aria-hidden='true'>⌄</span></button>
<p class='hn-title'>(62) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://rollingout.com/2026/03/31/oracle-slashes-30000-jobs-with-a-cold-6/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Oracle slashes 30k jobs</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>Oracle裁员3万</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47587935' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 907</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47587935' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 833</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--amber' data-tag='Business'>Business</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Oracle is cutting 30,000 jobs as part of a broad cost-cutting and restructuring effort amid cloud and ERP market shifts. The move underscores how big software vendors are recalibrating headcount to focus on core cloud platforms, with ripple effects for tech jobs, supplier ecosystems, and investor sentiment.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>甲骨文宣布裁减3万名员工，作为成本削减和云转型的一部分。此举凸显大型软件厂商在云平台优先级调整对技术工作、生态体系与投资者情绪的潜在影响。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://rollingout.com/wp-content/uploads/2025/09/shutterstock_2351343609-scaled-e1773175981701.jpg' data-full='https://rollingout.com/wp-content/uploads/2025/09/shutterstock_2351343609-scaled-e1773175981701.jpg' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>

<div class='hn-body'>
<button class='hn-digest-toggle' type='button' aria-expanded='false' title='Show summary and image' aria-label='Show summary and image'><span aria-hidden='true'>⌄</span></button>
<p class='hn-title'>(63) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://stevehanov.ca/blog/how-i-run-multiple-10k-mrr-companies-on-a-20month-tech-stack' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>I run multiple $10K MRR companies on a $20/month tech stack</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>用20美元/月的技术栈运营多家月订阅收入达一万美元的公司</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47736555' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 901</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47736555' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 488</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--amber' data-tag='Startups'>Startups</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Running several SaaS-like businesses on a $20/month stack is feasible with strong automation and lean operations. The post outlines strategies to maintain roughly $10K MRR per product at tiny ongoing costs, highlighting how a low-cost, automated stack can scale.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>在极低成本和高度自动化的条件下，仍然能经营多家以订阅为核心的产品并实现可观的月经常性收入。作者分享了将每个项目的月耗控在约20美元、个别产品实现约1万美元MRR的做法，挑战了对高成本技术栈的普遍认知。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://stevehanov.ca/blog/images/95f16b3dbf861a9156cb2c6d1be39f5e2fcfc1b1f4c5f11ee6f9e182bd4c5f5a.png' data-full='https://stevehanov.ca/blog/images/95f16b3dbf861a9156cb2c6d1be39f5e2fcfc1b1f4c5f11ee6f9e182bd4c5f5a.png' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-47647788' data-tags='Security,DevOps'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<button class='hn-digest-toggle' type='button' aria-expanded='false' title='Show summary and image' aria-label='Show summary and image'><span aria-hidden='true'>⌄</span></button>
<p class='hn-title'>(64) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://ergosphere.blog/posts/the-machines-are-fine/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>The threat is comfortable drift toward not understanding what you&#x27;re doing</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>威胁来自对所做之事逐渐变得难以理解的舒适漂移</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47647788' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 900</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47647788' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 587</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--red' data-tag='Security'>Security</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--indigo' data-tag='DevOps'>DevOps</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>A comfortable drift toward opaque automation can leave operators unaware of what their systems are actually doing. The piece argues that as tooling grows more capable, critical thinking and verification risk being outsourced to machines. Maintaining explicit mental models and observability is essential to reliability and security.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>对自动化日益隐蔽性的舒适感可能让人对系统实际在做什么失去清晰认知。文章强调，工具越强大，批判性思维与验证越易外包给机器，需保持可观测性与清晰的认知模型以维护可靠性与安全。</p>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-47648828' data-tags='AI'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<button class='hn-digest-toggle' type='button' aria-expanded='false' title='Show summary and image' aria-label='Show summary and image'><span aria-hidden='true'>⌄</span></button>
<p class='hn-title'>(65) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://lalitm.com/post/building-syntaqlite-ai/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Eight years of wanting, three months of building with AI</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>用 AI 耗时三个月打造 Syntaqlite AI，终成梦想</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47648828' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 888</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47648828' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 279</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--blue' data-tag='AI'>AI</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>After eight years of ambition, a developer builds Syntaqlite AI in three months using AI tools. The post showcases how AI can accelerate prototyping and tooling, turning a long-held dream into a tangible product quickly. It also highlights pragmatic tradeoffs and how to structure AI-assisted workflows.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>在八年的野心推动下，开发者用 AI 工具仅用三个月就打造出 Syntaqlite AI。此案例展示了 AI 如何显著加速原型开发和工具链搭建，让长期的梦想在短时间内变为现实。文中也讨论了实际取舍与如何组织 AI 辅助的工作流程。</p>
</div>
</div>
</div>

<div class='hn-body'>
<button class='hn-digest-toggle' type='button' aria-expanded='false' title='Show summary and image' aria-label='Show summary and image'><span aria-hidden='true'>⌄</span></button>
<p class='hn-title'>(66) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://developers.google.com/search/blog/2026/04/back-button-hijacking' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>A new spam policy for “back button hijacking”</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>防止后退按钮劫持的新策略</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47760764' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 888</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47760764' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 499</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--cyan' data-tag='Web'>Web</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Google updates its spam policy to crack down on back button hijacking, labeling manipulative UX that traps users as spam. The policy provides examples and enforcement expectations, encouraging developers to avoid overlays and deceptive navigation.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>谷歌更新了反垃圾邮件政策，打击利用后退按钮劫持的页面，视此类操纵性 UX 为垃圾信息。政策给出示例和执行预期，鼓励开发者避免覆盖层、强制导航等误导性设计。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://developers.google.com/static/search/blog/images/social-share-blog.png' data-full='https://developers.google.com/static/search/blog/images/social-share-blog.png' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-47933208' data-tags='Open Source,Privacy'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<button class='hn-digest-toggle' type='button' aria-expanded='false' title='Show summary and image' aria-label='Show summary and image'><span aria-hidden='true'>⌄</span></button>
<p class='hn-title'>(67) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://github.com/localsend/localsend' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Localsend: An open-source cross-platform alternative to AirDrop</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>Localsend：开源跨平台的 AirDrop 替代</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47933208' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 883</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47933208' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 267</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--green' data-tag='Open Source'>Open Source</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--red' data-tag='Privacy'>Privacy</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Localsend is an open-source, cross-platform alternative to AirDrop that lets devices share files over a local network without cloud traffic. It emphasizes privacy and platform-agnostic usability for Windows, macOS, and Linux users.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>Localsend 是一个开源、跨平台的 AirDrop 替代方案，支持在本地网络中直接传输文件，无需云端中转。面向 Windows、macOS、Linux 用户，强调隐私保护与无缝跨平台使用。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://repository-images.githubusercontent.com/578822531/6c3c4f46-0ab9-4737-9afe-7fa7f2f929d7' data-full='https://repository-images.githubusercontent.com/578822531/6c3c4f46-0ab9-4737-9afe-7fa7f2f929d7' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-47655408' data-tags='Show HN,AI,Education'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<button class='hn-digest-toggle' type='button' aria-expanded='false' title='Show summary and image' aria-label='Show summary and image'><span aria-hidden='true'>⌄</span></button>
<p class='hn-title'>(68) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://github.com/arman-bd/guppylm' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Show HN: I built a tiny LLM to demystify how language models work</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>Show HN：我构建了一个小型语言模型来揭示语言模型的工作原理</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47655408' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 880</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47655408' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 130</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--green' data-tag='Show HN'>Show HN</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--blue' data-tag='AI'>AI</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--sky' data-tag='Education'>Education</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>A tiny LLM demonstrates internal workings in an accessible way, touching on tokenization, attention, and prompt behavior. The project serves as a practical educational tool for developers curious about how large language models operate under the hood.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>一个小型语言模型以直观方式演示了分词、注意力等内部机制，帮助开发者直观理解大语言模型的工作原理。该项目强调从底层机制出发的教育价值。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://opengraph.githubassets.com/740a4eb42851a04a237a669f2d096c6a3a454f8d48f53b3a03c384c403da4c90/arman-bd/guppylm' data-full='https://opengraph.githubassets.com/740a4eb42851a04a237a669f2d096c6a3a454f8d48f53b3a03c384c403da4c90/arman-bd/guppylm' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-47757495' data-tags='Programming,DevOps'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<button class='hn-digest-toggle' type='button' aria-expanded='false' title='Show summary and image' aria-label='Show summary and image'><span aria-hidden='true'>⌄</span></button>
<p class='hn-title'>(69) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://github.github.com/gh-stack/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>GitHub Stacked PRs</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>GitHub 堆叠 PR</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47757495' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 880</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47757495' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 508</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--indigo' data-tag='Programming'>Programming</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--indigo' data-tag='DevOps'>DevOps</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Stacked PRs bundle several dependent pull requests into a single review flow, letting teams ship large features incrementally. The gh-stack tool provides commands to create, rebase, and track the stack, helping reviewers stay in sync across related changes.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>堆叠 PR 将若干相互依赖的 PR 打包到一个评审流程中，便于分阶段交付大型功能。gh-stack 提供创建、变基和跟踪等命令，使评审人能够在相关改动之间保持同步。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://github.github.com/gh-stack/_astro/stack-diagram.sUflwxUA_yKVNY.svg' data-full='https://github.github.com/gh-stack/_astro/stack-diagram.sUflwxUA_yKVNY.svg' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-47815774' data-tags='DevOps,Programming'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<button class='hn-digest-toggle' type='button' aria-expanded='false' title='Show summary and image' aria-label='Show summary and image'><span aria-hidden='true'>⌄</span></button>
<p class='hn-title'>(70) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://isayeter.com/posts/digitalocean-to-hetzner-migration/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Migrating from DigitalOcean to Hetzner</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>从 DigitalOcean 迁移到 Hetzner：零停机与成本下降</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47815774' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 879</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47815774' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 423</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--indigo' data-tag='DevOps'>DevOps</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--indigo' data-tag='Programming'>Programming</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>A hands-on migration showing how a production workload moved from DigitalOcean to Hetzner with zero downtime and significant cost savings. The piece shares practical steps, from plan and backups to DNS cutover and testing, illustrating how to optimize cloud spend without sacrificing availability.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>实践性案例展示了如何将生产 workloads 从 DigitalOcean 迁移到 Hetzner，确保零停机并显著降低成本。文章分享从计划、备份到 DNS 切换与测试的要点，说明在不牺牲可用性的前提下实现云成本优化。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://isayeter.com/images/domigration/45cf77e9709179874dbf5c259ae8b77207fb5f8932071f3db485b9ecade646f0.jpg' data-full='https://isayeter.com/images/domigration/45cf77e9709179874dbf5c259ae8b77207fb5f8932071f3db485b9ecade646f0.jpg' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-47612601' data-tags='Education'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<button class='hn-digest-toggle' type='button' aria-expanded='false' title='Show summary and image' aria-label='Show summary and image'><span aria-hidden='true'>⌄</span></button>
<p class='hn-title'>(71) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://undark.org/2026/04/01/sweden-schools-books/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Sweden goes back to basics, swapping screens for books in the classroom</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>瑞典在校内实验减少屏幕使用，课堂以书本取代屏幕</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47612601' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 873</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47612601' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 416</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--sky' data-tag='Education'>Education</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Sweden experiments with reducing screen time in schools to emphasize traditional reading and critical thinking, exploring impacts on attention, literacy, and learning outcomes. The approach signals a broader debate on how much technology belongs in classrooms and how to balance digital fluency with foundational skills.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>瑞典在校内实验减少屏幕使用，强调传统阅读和批判性思维，评估对注意力、读写能力与学习成效的影响。这一举措折射出对课堂中科技使用的更广泛辩论，以及数字素养与基礎技能之间的平衡。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://undark.org/wp-content/uploads/2026/04/GettyImages-1781953068_crop.jpg' data-full='https://undark.org/wp-content/uploads/2026/04/GettyImages-1781953068_crop.jpg' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-47868867' data-tags='Privacy,Security'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<button class='hn-digest-toggle' type='button' aria-expanded='false' title='Show summary and image' aria-label='Show summary and image'><span aria-hidden='true'>⌄</span></button>
<p class='hn-title'>(72) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://techcrunch.com/2026/04/22/apple-fixes-bug-that-cops-used-to-extract-deleted-chat-messages-from-iphones/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Apple fixes bug that cops used to extract deleted chat messages from iPhones</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>苹果修复漏洞，警方曾用其提取已删除的 iPhone 聊天记录</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47868867' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 869</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47868867' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 187</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--red' data-tag='Privacy'>Privacy</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--red' data-tag='Security'>Security</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Apple fixed a bug that allowed law enforcement to recover deleted iPhone chat messages, a rare privacy breach that persisted across devices. The patch closes this forensic loophole, reinforcing user data deletion and security. It underscores the ongoing tug-of-war between privacy protections and investigative tools in mobile platforms.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>苹果修复了一个以前被执法部门用来提取已删除 iPhone 聊天记录的漏洞。上述修补加强了消息删除的彻底性，提升了用户隐私的保护。此事再次凸显移动平台在隐私保护与执法需求之间的持续博弈。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://techcrunch.com/wp-content/uploads/2026/04/iphone-pop-up-notifications.jpg?resize=1200,900' data-full='https://techcrunch.com/wp-content/uploads/2026/04/iphone-pop-up-notifications.jpg?resize=1200,900' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>

<div class='hn-body'>

<p class='hn-title'>(73) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://www.bleepingcomputer.com/news/security/vercel-confirms-breach-as-hackers-claim-to-be-selling-stolen-data/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Vercel April 2026 security incident</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>Vercel 2026 年 4 月安全事件披露</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47824463' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 859</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47824463' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 486</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--red' data-tag='Security'>Security</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Vercel disclosed a security incident from April 2026, detailing what happened, the affected services, and the steps they took to contain and remediate. The disclosure underscores how even modern cloud platforms can face complex attacks, stressing the importance of monitoring, rapid incident response, and proactive customer credential hygiene.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>Vercel 披露了 2026 年 4 月的安全事件，说明了事件经过、受影响的服务以及应对和修复措施。这次披露凸显了即便是现代云平台也会遭遇复杂攻击，强调监控、快速响应和客户凭据安全的重要性。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://vercel.com/api/docs-og?title=Vercel%20April%202026%20security%20incident&amp;format=kb&amp;sig=61c47e5c0210e9c5e21b0833f72df07973dca32c7569ce19771da837cb76182a' data-full='https://vercel.com/api/docs-og?title=Vercel%20April%202026%20security%20incident&amp;format=kb&amp;sig=61c47e5c0210e9c5e21b0833f72df07973dca32c7569ce19771da837cb76182a' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>

<div class='hn-body'>
<button class='hn-digest-toggle' type='button' aria-expanded='false' title='Show summary and image' aria-label='Show summary and image'><span aria-hidden='true'>⌄</span></button>
<p class='hn-title'>(74) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://socket.dev/blog/bitwarden-cli-compromised' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Bitwarden CLI compromised in ongoing Checkmarx supply chain campaign</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>Bitwarden CLI 在持续的 Checkmarx 供应链攻击中被妥协</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47876043' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 857</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47876043' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 416</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--red' data-tag='Security'>Security</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Bitwarden CLI was compromised as part of a broader supply chain campaign tied to Checkmarx, illustrating how attackers weaponize developer toolchains. The incident underscores the risk of compromised updates and the need for strict verification of CLI binaries, signatures, and release channels.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>Bitwarden CLI 据称在与 Checkmarx 相关的供应链活动中被篡改，暴露了开发工具链的脆弱性。此事件强调应对 CLI 更新进行签名验证、固定版本、并监控 CI/CD 流水线中的异常更新。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://cdn.sanity.io/images/cgdhsj6q/production/65ef8dc5e66260e20fdf13cead82ebd41b705ee6-1018x666.png?w=1000&amp;q=95&amp;fit=max&amp;auto=format' data-full='https://cdn.sanity.io/images/cgdhsj6q/production/65ef8dc5e66260e20fdf13cead82ebd41b705ee6-1018x666.png?w=1000&amp;q=95&amp;fit=max&amp;auto=format' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>

<div class='hn-body'>

<p class='hn-title'>(75) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://apps.apple.com/nl/app/google-ai-edge-gallery/id6749645337' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Gemma 4 on iPhone</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>Gemma 4 在 iPhone 上</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47652561' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 851</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47652561' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 233</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--blue' data-tag='AI'>AI</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--red' data-tag='Privacy'>Privacy</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Gemma 4 is now available on iPhone, expanding access to Google&#x27;s Gemini model family on mobile devices. This move highlights the shift toward on-device AI, reducing cloud latency and improving privacy for everyday users.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>Gemma 4 已可在 iPhone 上使用，标志着 Gemini 家族在移动端的进一步扩展。此举凸显了将高性能 AI 推向设备端的趋势，提升隐私保护并降低延迟。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://is1-ssl.mzstatic.com/image/thumb/PurpleSource211/v4/a7/c4/20/a7c42022-eedc-887c-36c4-86270e1dcb93/Placeholder.mill/1200x630wa.jpg' data-full='https://is1-ssl.mzstatic.com/image/thumb/PurpleSource211/v4/a7/c4/20/a7c42022-eedc-887c-36c4-86270e1dcb93/Placeholder.mill/1200x630wa.jpg' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>

<p class='hn-title'>(76) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://www.thetimes.com/uk/technology-uk/article/sarah-wynn-williams-careless-people-meta-nrffdfpmf' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Author of &quot;Careless People&quot; banned from saying anything negative about Meta</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>《Careless People》作者被禁言，不能对 Meta 提出负评</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47639524' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 845</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47639524' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 553</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--orange' data-tag='Legal'>Legal</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>An author reportedly faces restrictions on saying anything negative about Meta due to a non-disparagement clause. The case highlights tensions around platform power, publishing rights, and freedom of expression in tech culture.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>据称该作者因“不得贬损”条款被限制公开批评 Meta。这起事件凸显了平台权力、出版权利与科技领域言论自由之间的紧张关系。</p>

<div class='hn-body'>
<button class='hn-digest-toggle' type='button' aria-expanded='false' title='Show summary and image' aria-label='Show summary and image'><span aria-hidden='true'>⌄</span></button>
<p class='hn-title'>(77) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://www.koshyjohn.com/blog/ai-should-elevate-your-thinking-not-replace-it/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>AI should elevate your thinking, not replace it</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>AI 应提升你的思考，而非替代它</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47913650' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 842</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47913650' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 584</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--blue' data-tag='AI'>AI</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>A thoughtful piece argues that AI should augment human thinking, not replace it, offering practical guidance on using AI as a cognitive partner while preserving critical judgment and accountability.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>文章主张 AI 应该提升人类思考，而非替代，提供把 AI 当作认知伙伴使用的实用方法，同时保留批判性判断与问责。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://www.koshyjohn.com/blog/ai-should-elevate-your-thinking-not-replace-it/blog_social.jpg' data-full='https://www.koshyjohn.com/blog/ai-should-elevate-your-thinking-not-replace-it/blog_social.jpg' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>

<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-47716043' data-tags='Open Source,Politics,Security'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<button class='hn-digest-toggle' type='button' aria-expanded='false' title='Show summary and image' aria-label='Show summary and image'><span aria-hidden='true'>⌄</span></button>
<p class='hn-title'>(78) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://www.numerique.gouv.fr/sinformer/espace-presse/souverainete-numerique-reduction-dependances-extra-europeennes/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>France Launches Government Linux Desktop Plan as Windows Exit Begins</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>法国启动政府级 Linux 桌面计划，Windows 退出进程开启</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47716043' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 841</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47716043' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 1</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--green' data-tag='Open Source'>Open Source</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--orange' data-tag='Politics'>Politics</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--red' data-tag='Security'>Security</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>France accelerates digital sovereignty by deploying Linux desktops across government as Windows migration begins. The move aims to cut vendor lock-in, reduce costs, and improve security, while posing challenges for app compatibility and procurement. It signals a broader trend toward open-source adoption in the public sector.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>法国通过在政府领域全面部署 Linux 桌面来提升数字主权，Windows 退出计划随之开启。此举旨在降低厂商锁定、压缩成本并提升安全性，但在应用兼容性与采购流程上也带来挑战。反映出公共部门日益重视开源的趋势。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://s3.eu-west-par.io.cloud.ovh.net/numerique-gouv-website/numerique-gouv-website/images/NUMERIQUE-GOUV_LOGO_PRIMAIRE_SIGNATURE_RVB.original.svg?X-Amz-Algorithm=AWS4-HMAC-SHA256&amp;X-Amz-Credential=83d53d2d1ca64211b6b9581adffa67ce%2F20260410%2Feu-west-par%2Fs3%2Faws4_request&amp;X-Amz-Date=20260410T132142Z&amp;X-Amz-Expires=3600&amp;X-Amz-SignedHeaders=host&amp;X-Amz-Signature=6f7e6dcdaa6aeb57fe15f60bb8e5564ce95029d31e0d93650bec45e36f7c10b8' data-full='https://s3.eu-west-par.io.cloud.ovh.net/numerique-gouv-website/numerique-gouv-website/images/NUMERIQUE-GOUV_LOGO_PRIMAIRE_SIGNATURE_RVB.original.svg?X-Amz-Algorithm=AWS4-HMAC-SHA256&amp;X-Amz-Credential=83d53d2d1ca64211b6b9581adffa67ce%2F20260410%2Feu-west-par%2Fs3%2Faws4_request&amp;X-Amz-Date=20260410T132142Z&amp;X-Amz-Expires=3600&amp;X-Amz-SignedHeaders=host&amp;X-Amz-Signature=6f7e6dcdaa6aeb57fe15f60bb8e5564ce95029d31e0d93650bec45e36f7c10b8' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>

<p class='hn-title'>(79) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://github.com/JuliusBrussee/caveman' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Caveman: Why use many token when few token do trick</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>Caveman：为何用多 Token 不如用少 Token 就能完成任务</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47647455' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 836</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47647455' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 351</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--blue' data-tag='AI'>AI</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Caveman argues that you can achieve effective results with far fewer tokens than conventional language models assume. The project demonstrates token-efficient techniques that reduce compute and cost while preserving performance.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>Caveman 主张在许多任务中无需大量标记即可取得良好效果，展示了更高效的标记方案以降低计算成本，同时保持性能。该项目提供了实现标记效率的具体方法与实践。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://opengraph.githubassets.com/bfdc2bea74554334d06bf058eff2c2a5b5ef92f12e7663e96b7d8e93d72e84ab/JuliusBrussee/caveman' data-full='https://opengraph.githubassets.com/bfdc2bea74554334d06bf058eff2c2a5b5ef92f12e7663e96b7d8e93d72e84ab/JuliusBrussee/caveman' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>

<p class='hn-title'>(80) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://dpa-international.com/general-news/urn:newsml:dpa.com:20090101:260430-930-14717/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Belgium stops decommissioning nuclear power plants</a></p>
