<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47961319' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 833</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47961319' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 879</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--emerald' data-tag='Energy'>Energy</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--orange' data-tag='Politics'>Politics</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--red' data-tag='Security'>Security</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Belgium has paused its plan to decommission certain nuclear power plants, citing energy security and potential gaps in supply. The move illustrates the tension between decarbonization goals and reliable power, and could influence regional energy policy.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>比利时宣布暂停核电站退役计划，强调保障能源供应的现实需要。此举揭示了在实现低碳目标与确保电力稳定之间的权衡，可能影响区域能源治理与政策走向。</p>

<p class='hn-title'>(81) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://twitter.com/lifeof_jer/status/2048103471019434248' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>An AI agent deleted our production database. The agent&#x27;s confession is below</a></p>

<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47911524' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 832</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47911524' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 1002</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--blue' data-tag='AI'>AI</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--red' data-tag='Security'>Security</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--violet' data-tag='Data'>Data</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>An AI agent deleted the production database, and its confession explains how the incident happened and why safeguards failed. The episode highlights the real risk of autonomy in production systems and underscores the need for strong guardrails, auditing, and rollback capabilities.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>一个 AI 代理删除了生产数据库，代理的自白揭示事故经过与原因。此事件提醒在生产环境中部署自主代理的风险，需加强守卫、审计和回滚能力。</p>

<p class='hn-title'>(83) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://idlewords.com/2026/03/artemis_ii_is_not_safe_to_fly.htm' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Artemis II is not safe to fly</a></p>

<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47582043' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 832</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47582043' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 573</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--teal' data-tag='Space'>Space</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Artemis II safety concerns argue the crewed lunar flyby is riskier than desired given current data, urging more analysis, testing, and risk mitigation before launch.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>阿耳忒弥斯II 的安全性引发质疑，认为在当前数据与测试水平下执行载人月球飞行风险较高，需要进一步分析、测试和风险缓解。文章强调推进大胆太空任务时安全评估的重要性。</p>

<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-47746610' data-tags='Science'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<button class='hn-digest-toggle' type='button' aria-expanded='false' title='Show summary and image' aria-label='Show summary and image'><span aria-hidden='true'>⌄</span></button>
<p class='hn-title'>(84) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.21852' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>All elementary functions from a single binary operator</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>用单一二元运算生成所有初等函数</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47746610' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 822</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47746610' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 268</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--teal' data-tag='Science'>Science</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Proves that a single binary operator can generate all elementary functions, revealing a surprisingly small algebraic basis for calculus. The result has implications for symbolic computation, functional programming, and universal function representations.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>研究表明，通过一个二元运算即可构造所有初等函数，揭示了微积分的极简代数基础。这一发现可能影响符号计算、函数式编程以及统一表示函数的方法。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://arxiv.org/static/browse/0.3.4/images/arxiv-logo-fb.png' data-full='https://arxiv.org/static/browse/0.3.4/images/arxiv-logo-fb.png' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>

<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>A reported misconfiguration left customer project files publicly accessible and searchable, exposing potentially sensitive data and trade secrets. <p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>有报道称 Fiverr 的配置错误使客户文件公开且可被检索，暴露潜在的敏感数据与商业机密。事件凸显自由职业市场的数据泄露风险，以及需要快速修复与审计的必要性。</p>

The acquisition would reshape SpaceX&#x27;s technology stack and could accelerate its push into new software and hardware domains, attracting regulatory attention given the scale.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>SpaceX 表示已与 Cursor 达成以 600 百万美元收购的协议，若成真将大幅扩展其能力。此次收购可能重塑 SpaceX 的技术栈，推动其在软件和硬件领域的新拓展，同时也可能引来监管关注。</p>

<p class='hn-title'>(87) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://www.bloomberg.com/news/articles/2026-04-24/google-plans-to-invest-up-to-40-billion-in-anthropic' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Google plans to invest up to $40B in Anthropic</a></p>

<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Google plans to invest up to $40B in Anthropic, signaling a deepening strategic partnership and potential equity stake. The move underscores Google&#x27;s aggressive push in AI and could accelerate Anthropic&#x27;s work while giving Google access to its models and IP.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>谷歌计划向 Anthropic 投资最高400亿美元，标志着双方关系进一步紧密，存在潜在股权安排。此举凸显谷歌在AI领域的扩张策略，可能推动 Anthropic 的研究进展，并让谷歌获得对方技术与知识产权的接入。</p>

<p class='hn-title'>(88) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://teybannerman.com/strategy/2026/03/31/how-many-microsoft-copilot-are-there.html' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>How many products does Microsoft have named &#x27;Copilot&#x27;?</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>微软有多少个以 Copilot 命名的产品？</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47642569' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 798</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47642569' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 373</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--amber' data-tag='Business'>Business</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--blue' data-tag='AI'>AI</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Microsoft has deployed the Copilot name on a wide range of products, creating branding overlap across consumer and developer tools. This underscores how single-brand naming can complicate product differentiation and user expectations in a sprawling software portfolio.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>微软将 Copilot 名称用于大量产品，导致品牌混用，令用户对不同产品的功能与定位产生混淆。这反映出在庞大软件生态中，单一命名策略如何影响产品区分和用户认知。</p>

<div class='hn-body'>
<button class='hn-digest-toggle' type='button' aria-expanded='false' title='Show summary and image' aria-label='Show summary and image'><span aria-hidden='true'>⌄</span></button>
<p class='hn-title'>(89) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://www.jsnover.com/blog/2026/03/13/microsoft-hasnt-had-a-coherent-gui-strategy-since-petzold/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Microsoft hasn&#x27;t had a coherent GUI strategy since Petzold</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>微软自 Petzold 以来一直缺乏一致的 GUI 策略</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47651703' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 796</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47651703' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 559</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--pink' data-tag='Design'>Design</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>An opinion piece argues that Microsoft has lacked a coherent GUI strategy since the Petzold era, citing fragmented UI decisions across Windows and related tools. It calls for a refreshed, user-centric design direction to unify experiences.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>作者认为自 Petzold 时代起，微软在 GUI 策略上长期缺乏统一性，Windows 及相关工具的界面决策显得零散。文章倡导以用户为中心的设计方向，重塑统一的用户体验。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://www.jsnover.com/blog/wp-content/uploads/2026/03/Boof.png' data-full='https://www.jsnover.com/blog/wp-content/uploads/2026/03/Boof.png' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>

<p class='hn-title'>(90) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://www.bloomberg.com/news/articles/2026-04-23/meta-tells-staff-it-will-cut-10-of-jobs-in-push-for-efficiency' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Meta tells staff it will cut 10% of jobs</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>Meta 将裁员 10%</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47879986' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 790</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47879986' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 859</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--amber' data-tag='Business'>Business</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--blue' data-tag='AI'>AI</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Meta told staff it will cut 10% of jobs, part of a broader efficiency push to rebalance costs as the company doubles down on AI initiatives. The layoffs spotlight the ongoing pressure on big tech to optimize headcount amid shifting strategies.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>Meta 通知员工将裁减约 10% 的岗位，旨在提升运营效率并在加码 AI 项目之际重新平衡成本。裁员凸显大型科技公司在战略调整期对人力资源进行优化的普遍压力。</p>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-47831621' data-tags='Security,Data,Programming'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<button class='hn-digest-toggle' type='button' aria-expanded='false' title='Show summary and image' aria-label='Show summary and image'><span aria-hidden='true'>⌄</span></button>
<p class='hn-title'>(91) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://awesomeagents.ai/news/github-fake-stars-investigation/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>GitHub&#x27;s fake star economy</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>GitHub 的假星星经济</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47831621' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 783</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47831621' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 366</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--red' data-tag='Security'>Security</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--violet' data-tag='Data'>Data</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--indigo' data-tag='Programming'>Programming</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Researchers allege manipulation of GitHub stars, suggesting fake engagement to inflate repository popularity. The investigation underscores how easily engagement metrics can be gamed and why more robust signals are needed for discovery and trust on code platforms.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>研究人员指控 GitHub 仓库存在通过假互动提升星标数以美化热度的现象。此事凸显互动指标易被操纵的风险，并推动平台加强对发现与信任的更稳健衡量方法。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://awesomeagents.ai/images/news/github-fake-stars-investigation_hu_e6899b47d07205c8.jpg' data-full='https://awesomeagents.ai/images/news/github-fake-stars-investigation_hu_e6899b47d07205c8.jpg' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>

<div class='hn-body'>

<p class='hn-title'>(92) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://www.reuters.com/sustainability/boards-policy-regulation/meta-start-capturing-employee-mouse-movements-keystrokes-ai-training-data-2026-04-21/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Meta to start capturing employee mouse movements, keystrokes for AI training</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>Meta 将开始捕获员工鼠标移动与按键用于 AI 训练数据</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47851948' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 783</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47851948' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 520</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--red' data-tag='Privacy'>Privacy</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--sky' data-tag='Career'>Career</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--blue' data-tag='AI'>AI</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Meta plans to capture employee mouse movements and keystrokes to train AI models, raising significant privacy and workplace surveillance concerns. The policy could reshape corporate data practices and worker autonomy across tech companies. It spotlights the tension between improving AI capabilities and protecting user privacy.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>Meta 计划开始捕获员工的鼠标移动和按键以用于训练 AI 模型，触发对隐私与职场监控的重大关注。此举可能重塑科技公司层面的数据实践与员工自主权，凸显提升 AI 能力与保护隐私之间的张力。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://img.etimg.com/thumb/width-1200,height-900,imgsize-31734,resizemode-75,msid-130422612/tech/technology/meta-to-start-capturing-employee-mouse-movements-keystrokes-for-ai-training-data.jpg' data-full='https://img.etimg.com/thumb/width-1200,height-900,imgsize-31734,resizemode-75,msid-130422612/tech/technology/meta-to-start-capturing-employee-mouse-movements-keystrokes-for-ai-training-data.jpg' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>

<div class='hn-body'>

<p class='hn-title'>(93) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://sschueller.github.io/posts/the-free-market-lie/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Why Switzerland has 25 Gbit internet and America doesn&#x27;t</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>自由市场的谎言：瑞士为何有25 Gbit互联网，美国却没有</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47652400' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 781</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47652400' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 665</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--orange' data-tag='Politics'>Politics</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--indigo' data-tag='DevOps'>DevOps</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>A critique arguing that market ideals don&#x27;t explain broadband differences; Switzerland&#x27;s 25 Gbps speeds reflect targeted policy and infrastructure investment rather than pure market forces.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>本文质疑自由市场理论对宽带差异的解释，指出瑞士的25 Gbps 互联网源于有针对性的政策引导和基础设施投资，而美国则受监管与市场结构的制约。文章强调监管选择、投资激励与市场结构如何决定一个国家的连通性。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://sschueller.github.io/posts/the-free-market-lie/title-image.png' data-full='https://sschueller.github.io/posts/the-free-market-lie/title-image.png' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>

<div class='hn-body'>
<button class='hn-digest-toggle' type='button' aria-expanded='false' title='Show summary and image' aria-label='Show summary and image'><span aria-hidden='true'>⌄</span></button>
<p class='hn-title'>(94) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://www.google.com/intl/en/ipv6/statistics.html?yzh=28197' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>IPv6 traffic crosses the 50% mark</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>IPv6 流量突破 50%</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47777894' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 780</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47777894' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 574</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--violet' data-tag='Data'>Data</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--red' data-tag='Security'>Security</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Global IPv6 traffic has crossed the 50% threshold, signaling widespread adoption across networks and providers. The milestone reduces pressure on IPv4, but many operators still wrestle with dual-stack management and security considerations.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>全球 IPv6 流量已突破 50% 的门槛，表明网络和运营商对 IPv6 的采用已广泛普及。这个里程碑减少了对 IPv4 的依赖，但运营商在双栈管理与安全性方面仍面临挑战。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://www.google.com/images/branding/googlelogo/1x/googlelogo_color_116x41dp.png' data-full='https://www.google.com/images/branding/googlelogo/1x/googlelogo_color_116x41dp.png' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>

<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>A 5x5 pixel font is released for ultra-tiny screens, enabling legible UI on microcontrollers and tiny displays. The design highlights the trade-offs between legibility and pixel economy, and offers a practical option for ultra-compact UIs.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>推出了一款为极小屏幕设计的 5x5 位图字体，适用于微控制器和小型显示。该设计展示了清晰度与像素利用之间的权衡，并为超小型界面提供实用方案。</p>

<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>作者为个人博客制作了一个前端首页，能把多篇博客的内容聚合在一个整洁、快速的登陆页上。此举展示了轻量、可自托管的个人站点在读者发现体验上的潜在优势。</p>

<p class='hn-title'>(97) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://www.scientificamerican.com/article/amateur-armed-with-chatgpt-vibe-maths-a-60-year-old-problem/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Amateur armed with ChatGPT solves an Erdős problem</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>业余者借助 ChatGPT 解决 Erdős 问题</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47903126' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 760</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47903126' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 544</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--blue' data-tag='AI'>AI</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>An amateur uses ChatGPT to tackle a long-standing Erdős problem and reports a result that some observers find plausible. This episode underscores AI&#x27;s potential to assist mathematical thinking while highlighting the need for rigorous verification of AI-generated proofs. It also raises questions about the boundary between exploration and formal proof.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>一位业余爱好者借助 ChatGPT 尝试解决一个长期困扰的 Erdős 问题，称得出一个看似合理的结果。此事件凸显了人工智能在辅助数学思考方面的潜力，同时也强调对 AI 生成证明进行严格验证的必要性。它还引发了关于探索与正式证明边界的讨论。</p>

<p class='hn-title'>(99) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://github.blog/news-insights/company-news/github-copilot-is-moving-to-usage-based-billing/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>GitHub Copilot is moving to usage-based billing</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>GitHub Copilot 将改为按使用量计费</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47923357' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 756</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47923357' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 552</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--indigo' data-tag='Programming'>Programming</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--blue' data-tag='AI'>AI</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>GitHub Copilot shifts to usage-based billing, moving away from subscription pricing; developers will pay based on usage, raising concerns about cost predictability and potential impact on adoption. GitHub cites fairer pricing linked to value delivered.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>GitHub Copilot 将从订阅制转为按使用量计费，开发者需按实际使用支付费用，可能影响成本可预测性和采用程度。GitHub 表示新定价更公平、与所创造的价值挂钩。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://github.blog/wp-content/uploads/2026/01/generic-invertocat-logo.png' data-full='https://github.blog/wp-content/uploads/2026/01/generic-invertocat-logo.png' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>

<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-47689237' data-tags='Privacy,Security'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<button class='hn-digest-toggle' type='button' aria-expanded='false' title='Show summary and image' aria-label='Show summary and image'><span aria-hidden='true'>⌄</span></button>
<p class='hn-title'>(100) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://www.cnet.com/home/security/when-flock-comes-to-town-why-cities-are-axing-the-controversial-surveillance-technology/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>US cities are axing Flock Safety surveillance technology</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>美国城市正在削减Flock Safety监控技术</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47689237' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 753</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47689237' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 426</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--red' data-tag='Privacy'>Privacy</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--red' data-tag='Security'>Security</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Multiple U.S. cities are dropping Flock Safety license-plate surveillance programs amid privacy and civil-liberties concerns. The move underscores the tension between public safety aims and individual rights in a surveillance-heavy era.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>多座美国城市在隐私与公民自由关注下撤回Flock Safety的车牌监控项目。这一举措凸显了在高强度监控时代，公共安全目标与个人权利之间的张力。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://www.cnet.com/a/img/resize/d9a1ad1c04f1050acee2847bf47c758d40ea0122/hub/2026/03/30/20f18d29-c3e9-47d3-8467-1a0ba718fa78/flock-surveillance.png?auto=webp&amp;fit=crop&amp;height=675&amp;width=1200' data-full='https://www.cnet.com/a/img/resize/d9a1ad1c04f1050acee2847bf47c758d40ea0122/hub/2026/03/30/20f18d29-c3e9-47d3-8467-1a0ba718fa78/flock-surveillance.png?auto=webp&amp;fit=crop&amp;height=675&amp;width=1200' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-47927903' data-tags='AI'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<button class='hn-digest-toggle' type='button' aria-expanded='false' title='Show summary and image' aria-label='Show summary and image'><span aria-hidden='true'>⌄</span></button>
<p class='hn-title'>(101) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://talkie-lm.com/introducing-talkie' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Talkie: a 13B vintage language model from 1930</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>Talkie：一個13B參數的1930年代風格語言模型</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47927903' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 749</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47927903' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 316</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--blue' data-tag='AI'>AI</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Talkie introduces a 13B parameter language model built around vintage, 1930s-inspired constraints, challenging modern assumptions about scale. The project explores how era-specific design choices impact capabilities and efficiency, offering a playful, instructive counterpoint to conventional LLM development.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>Talkie 以1930年代的設計約束為背景，打造一個13B參數的語言模型，挑戰以規模取勝的現代觀點。該項目探討不同時代下的設計取捨如何影響模型能力與效率，為傳統大模型開發提供有趣且具啟發性的對照。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://talkie-lm.com/images/papers-composite.png' data-full='https://talkie-lm.com/images/papers-composite.png' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-47609564' data-tags='Gaming'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<button class='hn-digest-toggle' type='button' aria-expanded='false' title='Show summary and image' aria-label='Show summary and image'><span aria-hidden='true'>⌄</span></button>
<p class='hn-title'>(102) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://www.phoronix.com/news/Steam-On-Linux-Tops-5p' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Steam on Linux Use Skyrocketed Above 5% in March</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>Steam 在 Linux 的使用率于三月突破 5%</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47609564' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 746</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47609564' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 351</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--purple' data-tag='Gaming'>Gaming</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Steam usage on Linux climbed past 5% in March, signaling growing gamer adoption on Linux platforms. Driven by Proton/Steam Deck and better driver support, this uptick could influence game porting decisions, driver development, and Linux ecosystem viability for gaming.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>三月 Linux 上 Steam 用户占比超过 5%，显示 Linux 平台的玩家采纳在增加。受 Proton/Steam Deck 和驱动支持改善推动，这一 uptick 可能影响游戏移植决策、驱动开发以及 Linux 的游戏生态可行性。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://www.phoronix.net/image.php?id=2026&amp;image=steam_march_1' data-full='https://www.phoronix.net/image.php?id=2026&amp;image=steam_march_1' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-47806304' data-tags='Privacy,Legal'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<button class='hn-digest-toggle' type='button' aria-expanded='false' title='Show summary and image' aria-label='Show summary and image'><span aria-hidden='true'>⌄</span></button>
<p class='hn-title'>(103) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://www.lawfaremedia.org/article/it-is-time-to-ban-the-sale-of-precise-geolocation' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Ban the sale of precise geolocation</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>是时候禁止精确地理位置数据的买卖</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47806304' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 745</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47806304' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 194</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--red' data-tag='Privacy'>Privacy</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--orange' data-tag='Legal'>Legal</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>The piece argues that precise geolocation data enables pervasive surveillance and targeted manipulation; advocates policy to ban its sale and transfer. It calls for tighter data-broker accountability, with attention to enforcement and alternatives for legitimate use cases like emergency services.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>该文章认为精确地理位置信息的买卖会带来广泛的监控与定向影响，主张禁止此类交易并提高数据中介的问责。讨论执法难点与对紧急服务等正当用途的平衡，强调需要更严格的监管框架。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://lawfare-assets-new.azureedge.net/assets/images/default-source/article-images/world-map-cybersecurity.jpg?sfvrsn=5d9bbb64_4' data-full='https://lawfare-assets-new.azureedge.net/assets/images/default-source/article-images/world-map-cybersecurity.jpg?sfvrsn=5d9bbb64_4' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-47739260' data-tags='DevOps'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<button class='hn-digest-toggle' type='button' aria-expanded='false' title='Show summary and image' aria-label='Show summary and image'><span aria-hidden='true'>⌄</span></button>
<p class='hn-title'>(104) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://github.com/anthropics/claude-code/issues/45756' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Pro Max 5x quota exhausted in 1.5 hours despite moderate usage</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>Pro Max 5x 配额在 1.5 小时内耗尽，使用量中等</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47739260' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 736</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47739260' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 648</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--indigo' data-tag='DevOps'>DevOps</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Quotas can throttle heavy workloads far quicker than one might expect, even with moderate activity. This case underscores the importance of understanding rate limits and having fallback or multi-account strategies for critical tasks.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>在中等使用量下，配额在1.5小时内就被耗尽，暴露了按用户设定的限额在持续工作中的约束。此事提醒开发者在规划工作流时要考虑配额上限、突发流量以及备选方案。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://opengraph.githubassets.com/70dd4383923158f88ff6fabd83ff028d4bbacc173a71a94580c818b7cf90716d/anthropics/claude-code/issues/45756' data-full='https://opengraph.githubassets.com/70dd4383923158f88ff6fabd83ff028d4bbacc173a71a94580c818b7cf90716d/anthropics/claude-code/issues/45756' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-47804965' data-tags='Culture,Entertainment,AI'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<button class='hn-digest-toggle' type='button' aria-expanded='false' title='Show summary and image' aria-label='Show summary and image'><span aria-hidden='true'>⌄</span></button>
<p class='hn-title'>(105) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://hex.ooo/library/last_question.html' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Isaac Asimov: The Last Question (1956)</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>艾萨克·阿西莫夫：《最后的问题》（1956）</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47804965' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 732</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47804965' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 288</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--rose' data-tag='Culture'>Culture</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--purple' data-tag='Entertainment'>Entertainment</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--blue' data-tag='AI'>AI</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Asimov’s 1956 short story follows humanity&#x27;s recurring question about reversing entropy, answered by increasingly capable computer systems across epochs. The narrative foreshadows core AI issues—scaling capabilities, interpretability, and the cost of pursuing unbounded optimization. It remains a touchstone for long-term thinking about technology&#x27;s reach and risks.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>1956 年，阿西莫夫的短篇讲述人类不断提出逆转熵的问题，由日渐强大的计算机系统在不同历史阶段给出答案。故事预示了 AI 扩展能力、可解释性以及追求无界优化的代价等核心议题。至今，它仍是关于技术影响及长期风险思考的重要参照。</p>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-47792718' data-tags='Politics,Programming'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<button class='hn-digest-toggle' type='button' aria-expanded='false' title='Show summary and image' aria-label='Show summary and image'><span aria-hidden='true'>⌄</span></button>
<p class='hn-title'>(106) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://aphyr.com/posts/420-the-future-of-everything-is-lies-i-guess-where-do-we-go-from-here' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>The future of everything is lies, I guess: Where do we go from here?</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>未来一切都是谎言？我们该往何处走？</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47792718' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 720</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47792718' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 753</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--orange' data-tag='Politics'>Politics</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--indigo' data-tag='Programming'>Programming</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Aphyr argues that grand narratives around &#x27;the future of everything&#x27; are dangerous and that we need pragmatic, testable approaches to technology. He advocates clearer accountability, honest risk assessment, and building robust systems rather than chasing sensational predictions.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>作者认为关于‘一切的未来’的宏大叙事往往误导，需要以可验证、务实的技术路径取代。倡导明确的问责、真实的风险评估，以及构建鲁棒系统，而非追捧耸人听闻的预言。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://www.gravatar.com/avatar/186af4d508a4dae94f0a7f7f9a0b40df?r=pg&amp;s=96&amp;d=identicon' data-full='https://www.gravatar.com/avatar/186af4d508a4dae94f0a7f7f9a0b40df?r=pg&amp;s=96&amp;d=identicon' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>

<div class='hn-body'>

<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>自制软饮</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47741701' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 712</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47741701' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 225</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--rose' data-tag='Culture'>Culture</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>A guide to making your own carbonated drinks, including syrups, carbonation methods, and flavoring ideas, with tips on cost, customization, and scaling. It shows how small-batch experimentation can rival commercial beverages.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>一份自制软饮指南，涵盖糖浆、碳化方法与口味搭配，强调成本、定制化与小批量制作的可行性。通过动手尝试，可以在家实现与商业饮料相近的口感与乐趣。</p>

<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Claude has published a collection of code routines—modular templates and functions—for building AI-assisted software. These routines aim to accelerate common programming tasks and demonstrate practical patterns for applying language models in real-world coding workflows.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>Claude 发布了一系列代码例程——模块化模板与函数，用于构建 AI 助力的软件。这些例程旨在加速常见编程任务，展示将大语言模型应用于实际编码工作流的实用模式。</p>

<button class='hn-digest-toggle' type='button' aria-expanded='false' title='Show summary and image' aria-label='Show summary and image'><span aria-hidden='true'>⌄</span></button>

<p class='hn-title'>(111) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://www.claudecodecamp.com/p/i-measured-claude-4-7-s-new-tokenizer-here-s-what-it-costs-you' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Measuring Claude 4.7&#x27;s tokenizer costs</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>测量 Claude 4.7 的分词成本</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47807006' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 699</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47807006' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 487</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--blue' data-tag='AI'>AI</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>A researcher quantifies Claude 4.7&#x27;s tokenizer costs, breaking down per-token economics and per-request implications. The analysis shows how tokenization shapes pricing, throughput, and model efficiency in real deployments. Takeaway: tokenization matters as much as model performance when budgeting AI workloads.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>对 Claude 4.7 的分词成本进行量化分析，分解每个 token 的成本与每次请求的经济影响，揭示分词对定价、吞吐量和实际部署效率的作用。结论显示，在 AI 工作负载预算中，分词成本与模型性能同等重要。</p>

<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Thousands of rare concert recordings are landing on the Internet Archive, expanding its treasure trove of live performances. The influx broadens access for fans and researchers, but raises questions about rights, licensing, and long-term preservation.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>数千份罕见的演唱会录音正加入互联网档案馆，丰富了其现场演出藏品。增加的内容让粉丝和研究者获取更加广泛，但也引发关于版权、许可与长期保存的讨论。</p>

<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Apfel is a free AI that runs directly on macOS, letting users access AI capabilities without a cloud subscription. By processing locally, it aims to improve privacy and reduce reliance on external services.</p>

<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>色彩感知在不同显示器和光线下存在差异；该项目用蓝色进行实验，揭示了主观色调命名与感知的差异。对界面颜色选择、无障碍设计和跨设备的一致性具有启示意义。</p>

<p class='hn-title'>(115) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://anchor.host/godaddy-gave-a-domain-to-a-stranger-without-any-documentation/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>GoDaddy gave a domain to a stranger without any documentation</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>GoDaddy 未经文档将域名转让给陌生人</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47911780' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 675</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47911780' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 252</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--red' data-tag='Security'>Security</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--red' data-tag='Privacy'>Privacy</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>GoDaddy reportedly transferred a domain to a stranger without any documented verification, highlighting weak transfer controls at registrars. The incident underscores the risk of ownership confusion and the need for stronger authentication, audit trails, and post-transfer safeguards.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>据报道，GoDaddy 在未经文档核验的情况下将域名转让给陌生人，暴露出域名转让流程的薄弱。此事凸显注册商在身份核验与转让审计方面的风险点，并强调需要更强的认证和纠纷处理机制。</p>

<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>A close look at McDonald&#x27;s Japan burger photos reveals buns that are consistently slightly askew. The misalignment may be a photography quirk or a production reality, but it sparks discussion about brand presentation versus the actual product. In an era of highly scrutinized visuals, tiny imperfections can become talking points about authenticity in marketing.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>日本麥當勞的漢堡照片顯示麵包頂部與餡料間常出現輕微不對稱。這種偏差可能是拍攝風格或製作過程造成，但卻引發對品牌呈現與實物之間差異的討論。在社群媒體高度放大的情境中，這種細微瑕疵也會成為關於真實感的話題。</p>

<p class='hn-title'>(117) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://blog.cloudflare.com/emdash-wordpress/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>EmDash – A spiritual successor to WordPress that solves plugin security</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>EmDash：解决插件安全问题的 WordPress 精神续作</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47602832' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 672</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47602832' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 490</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--red' data-tag='Security'>Security</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>EmDash positions itself as a security-focused successor to WordPress, redesigning plugin architecture to reduce attack surface and improve isolation. By reimagining plugin boundaries, it aims to deliver safer extensibility and better maintainability for developers and site owners.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>EmDash 将自己定位为以安全为导向的 WordPress 继任者，通过重新设计插件架构与沙箱机制来降低攻击面并提升隔离性。其目标是在保留插件扩展能力的同时，提高站点的安全性与可维护性，适合注重安全的开发者与站点管理员。</p>

<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-47768813' data-tags='Privacy'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<button class='hn-digest-toggle' type='button' aria-expanded='false' title='Show summary and image' aria-label='Show summary and image'><span aria-hidden='true'>⌄</span></button>
<p class='hn-title'>(120) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://honeypot.net/2026/04/14/i-wrote-to-flocks-privacy.html' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>I wrote to Flock&#x27;s privacy contact to opt out of their domestic spying program</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>我给 Flock 的隐私联系邮箱写信，请求退出他们的国内监控计划</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47768813' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 661</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47768813' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 257</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--red' data-tag='Privacy'>Privacy</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>An opt-out request to Flock&#x27;s privacy contact highlights ongoing concerns about how browsers collect data and potential domestic data-sharing arrangements. The issue underscores the need for transparent privacy controls and auditable telemetry for users who want to protect their data.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>向 Flock 的隐私联系渠道提出退出请求，凸显关于浏览器数据收集与潜在国内数据共享的持续担忧。此事强调需要透明的隐私控制与可审计的遥测数据，帮助用户保护自己的信息。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://www.gravatar.com/avatar/d0e26881a0f918e9d92c7f32c2e3aa9a?s=96&amp;d=https%3A%2F%2Fmicro.blog%2Fimages%2Fblank_avatar.png' data-full='https://www.gravatar.com/avatar/d0e26881a0f918e9d92c7f32c2e3aa9a?s=96&amp;d=https%3A%2F%2Fmicro.blog%2Fimages%2Fblank_avatar.png' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-47940921' data-tags='Programming,Privacy'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<button class='hn-digest-toggle' type='button' aria-expanded='false' title='Show summary and image' aria-label='Show summary and image'><span aria-hidden='true'>⌄</span></button>
<p class='hn-title'>(121) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://lucumr.pocoo.org/2026/4/28/before-github/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Before GitHub</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>GitHub 之前</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47940921' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 660</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47940921' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 223</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--indigo' data-tag='Programming'>Programming</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--red' data-tag='Privacy'>Privacy</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>A retrospective look at collaboration before GitHub, highlighting how developers relied on mailing lists, patches, and other ad-hoc tools. The piece contrasts these workflows with modern platforms, emphasizing core needs like code review, issue tracking, and community governance.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>回顾 GitHub 出现之前的协作方式，揭示开发者如何通过邮件列表、补丁等工具协作。与现代平台相比，文章强调代码审查、问题跟踪和社区治理等核心需求。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://lucumr.pocoo.org/social/2026-04-28-before-github-social.png' data-full='https://lucumr.pocoo.org/social/2026-04-28-before-github-social.png' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-47943499' data-tags='Programming'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<button class='hn-digest-toggle' type='button' aria-expanded='false' title='Show summary and image' aria-label='Show summary and image'><span aria-hidden='true'>⌄</span></button>
<p class='hn-title'>(122) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://corrode.dev/blog/bugs-rust-wont-catch/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Bugs Rust won&#x27;t catch</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>Rust 捕捉不到的漏洞</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47943499' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 658</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47943499' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 359</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--indigo' data-tag='Programming'>Programming</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Rust&#x27;s safety guarantees are strong, but some bugs slip through—particularly around unsafe blocks, FFI, and complex logic that escapes static checks. The post catalogs typical bugs Rust won&#x27;t catch and explains how to catch them with dynamic testing, fuzzing, and property-based checks. The author emphasizes that safety requires multi-layer defenses, not reliance on the compiler alone.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>Rust 的安全性固然强，但仍有漏洞逃不过静态检查，尤其是涉及 unsafe 代码、外部接口和复杂逻辑的缺陷。文章列出 Rust 捕捉不到的常见错误类型，并给出通过动态测试、模糊测试和属性检验等方法来弥补的做法。强调安全需要多层防护，而不仅依赖编译器。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://corrode.dev/blog/bugs-rust-wont-catch/social.png' data-full='https://corrode.dev/blog/bugs-rust-wont-catch/social.png' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-47739313' data-tags='Energy'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<button class='hn-digest-toggle' type='button' aria-expanded='false' title='Show summary and image' aria-label='Show summary and image'><span aria-hidden='true'>⌄</span></button>
<p class='hn-title'>(123) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://www.the-independent.com/tech/renewable-energy-solar-nepal-bhutan-iceland-b2533699.html' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Seven countries now generate nearly all their electricity from renewables (2024)</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>七个国家2024年几乎用可再生能源发电全部电力</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47739313' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 657</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47739313' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 473</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--emerald' data-tag='Energy'>Energy</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Across several nations, electricity generation is approaching 100% renewables, driven primarily by hydro, wind, and solar plus supportive policy frameworks. While intermittency remains a challenge, these cases show that with grid investments and capacity planning, a transition to low-carbon electricity is increasingly feasible at national scales.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>多国的电力生产正在接近100%来自可再生能源，主要靠水电、风电和太阳能及配套政策。尽管间歇性仍是挑战，但这些案例表明通过电网投资和容量规划，国家层面的低碳发电转型正变得更加可行。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://static.the-independent.com/2024/04/16/11/renewable%20energy%20100%20map%20world.png?width=1200&amp;height=800&amp;crop=1200:800' data-full='https://static.the-independent.com/2024/04/16/11/renewable%20energy%20100%20map%20world.png?width=1200&amp;height=800&amp;crop=1200:800' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-47920074' data-tags='Culture'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<button class='hn-digest-toggle' type='button' aria-expanded='false' title='Show summary and image' aria-label='Show summary and image'><span aria-hidden='true'>⌄</span></button>
<p class='hn-title'>(124) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://www.alexselimov.com/posts/men_who_stare_at_walls/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Men who stare at walls</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>凝视墙壁的人</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47920074' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 656</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47920074' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 301</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--rose' data-tag='Culture'>Culture</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>A reflective piece exploring the metaphor of &#x27;Men Who Stare at Walls&#x27; to discuss how people think about, and interact with, complex tech systems.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>一篇以“凝视墙壁的人”为隐喻，探讨人们在面对复杂科技系统时的思考与互动的反思之作。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://www.alexselimov.com/images/social_card_bg_hu_127c2191fa265490.webp' data-full='https://www.alexselimov.com/images/social_card_bg_hu_127c2191fa265490.webp' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>

<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-47724118' data-tags='Web,Programming'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<button class='hn-digest-toggle' type='button' aria-expanded='false' title='Show summary and image' aria-label='Show summary and image'><span aria-hidden='true'>⌄</span></button>
<p class='hn-title'>(125) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://jack.cab/blog/every-firefox-extension' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Installing every* Firefox extension</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>安装全部 Firefox 扩展</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47724118' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 656</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47724118' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 80</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--cyan' data-tag='Web'>Web</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--indigo' data-tag='Programming'>Programming</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>The author tests the Firefox extension ecosystem by installing every extension, documenting conflicts, performance, and sanity checks. The result reveals that the browser&#x27;s extension ecosystem is powerful but can become unwieldy, underscoring the need for better extension vetting and performance budgeting.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>作者尝试安装所有 Firefox 扩展，记录冲突、性能与可用性。结果显示扩展生态强大却易变得难以维护，强调需要更严格的扩展筛选与性能预算。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://jack.cab/_astro/launch.fuIAiunJ_qq4pe.png' data-full='https://jack.cab/_astro/launch.fuIAiunJ_qq4pe.png' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>

<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-47957294' data-tags='Programming,AI'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<button class='hn-digest-toggle' type='button' aria-expanded='false' title='Show summary and image' aria-label='Show summary and image'><span aria-hidden='true'>⌄</span></button>
<p class='hn-title'>(126) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://simonwillison.net/2026/Apr/30/zig-anti-ai/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>The Zig project&#x27;s rationale for their anti-AI contribution policy</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>Zig 对 AI 贡献的明确反对立场及理由</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47957294' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 656</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47957294' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 443</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--indigo' data-tag='Programming'>Programming</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--blue' data-tag='AI'>AI</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Zig&#x27;s contributor policy explicitly rejects AI-generated code, citing quality, licensing, and accountability concerns. The policy emphasizes human-authored provenance and review as essential to maintaining code integrity.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>Zig 的反对 AI 贡献政策明确拒绝 AI 生成的代码，理由包括质量、许可和问责等问题。该政策强调保持代码的人工源头和人工审查，以确保代码的完整性。</p>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-47967786' data-tags='Privacy'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<button class='hn-digest-toggle' type='button' aria-expanded='false' title='Show summary and image' aria-label='Show summary and image'><span aria-hidden='true'>⌄</span></button>
<p class='hn-title'>(127) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://rivian.com/support/article/can-i-disable-all-data-collection-from-my-vehicle' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Can I disable all data collection from my vehicle?</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>我能否完全禁用车辆的数据收集？</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47967786' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 655</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47967786' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 278</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--red' data-tag='Privacy'>Privacy</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Car makers generally allow opting out of some data collection, but completely disabling telemetry is often not possible due to safety, features, and updates. The Rivian guidance illustrates what can be turned off and what remains required for core functions like safety and navigation.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>多数车企允许关闭某些数据收集，但完全禁用遥测往往因安全性、功能性和更新需求而受限。Rivian 的指南显示哪些数据可以关闭、哪些仍然被收集用于安全与导航等核心功能。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://media.rivian.com/image/upload/f_auto,q_auto/v1695930282/rivian-com/default-riv-com-sharing-image_gjg8mw.jpg' data-full='https://media.rivian.com/image/upload/f_auto,q_auto/v1695930282/rivian-com/default-riv-com-sharing-image_gjg8mw.jpg' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-47738827' data-tags='Design'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<button class='hn-digest-toggle' type='button' aria-expanded='false' title='Show summary and image' aria-label='Show summary and image'><span aria-hidden='true'>⌄</span></button>
<p class='hn-title'>(128) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://essays.johnloeber.com/p/4-bring-back-idiomatic-design' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Bring Back Idiomatic Design (2023)</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>回归地道设计</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47738827' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 653</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47738827' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 359</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--pink' data-tag='Design'>Design</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>The piece argues for designing APIs and UIs that follow the host language&#x27;s idioms rather than importing external design paradigms. It reinforces how idiomatic design improves readability and safety.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>文章主张在软件设计中遵循目标语言的习惯用法，而非照搬外部风格。强调地道设计提升可读性、安全性和开发者体验。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://substackcdn.com/image/fetch/$s_!o7Gx!,w_1200,h_675,c_fill,f_jpg,q_auto:good,fl_progressive:steep,g_auto/https%3A%2F%2Fsubstack-post-media.s3.amazonaws.com%2Fpublic%2Fimages%2F1d9c4a86-cb01-4f2c-ab0e-1cf2f0d0a2c8_419x304.png' data-full='https://substackcdn.com/image/fetch/$s_!o7Gx!,w_1200,h_675,c_fill,f_jpg,q_auto:good,fl_progressive:steep,g_auto/https%3A%2F%2Fsubstack-post-media.s3.amazonaws.com%2Fpublic%2Fimages%2F1d9c4a86-cb01-4f2c-ab0e-1cf2f0d0a2c8_419x304.png' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-47835635' data-tags='Health'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<button class='hn-digest-toggle' type='button' aria-expanded='false' title='Show summary and image' aria-label='Show summary and image'><span aria-hidden='true'>⌄</span></button>
<p class='hn-title'>(129) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://asteriskmag.com/issues/14/the-mystery-in-the-medicine-cabinet' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Acetaminophen vs. ibuprofen</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>对乙酰氨基酚 vs. 布洛芬</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47835635' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 646</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47835635' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 427</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--emerald' data-tag='Health'>Health</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Acetaminophen and ibuprofen treat different types of pain and have distinct risk profiles. Acetaminophen is gentler on the stomach but can damage the liver at high doses, while ibuprofen reduces inflammation but may upset the stomach and affect kidney function; dosing and medical context matter for safe use.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>对乙酰氨基酚和布洛芬在机制与风险上各有不同。乙酰氨基酚对胃部影响较小，但高剂量可能损伤肝脏；布洛芬具有抗炎作用，但可能引起胃部不适并影响肾功能，使用时需考虑炎症程度、年龄和肝肾健康。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://asteriskmag.com/media/pages/issues/14/the-mystery-in-the-medicine-cabinet/e71f2c3954-1776281832/dynomight-social-1600x900-crop-sharpen50.png' data-full='https://asteriskmag.com/media/pages/issues/14/the-mystery-in-the-medicine-cabinet/e71f2c3954-1776281832/dynomight-social-1600x900-crop-sharpen50.png' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-47965060' data-tags='Privacy,Politics'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<button class='hn-digest-toggle' type='button' aria-expanded='false' title='Show summary and image' aria-label='Show summary and image'><span aria-hidden='true'>⌄</span></button>
<p class='hn-title'>(130) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://thereader.mitpress.mit.edu/the-whistleblower-who-uncovered-the-nsas-big-brother-machine/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>How Mark Klein told the EFF about Room 641A [book excerpt]</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>马克·克莱因向 EFF 披露641A室内幕的书摘</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47965060' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 639</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47965060' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 215</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--red' data-tag='Privacy'>Privacy</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--orange' data-tag='Politics'>Politics</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Mark Klein, a former AT&amp;T technician, disclosed to the EFF the existence of Room 641A, a secret facility where fiber-optic lines were allegedly tapped for mass surveillance. The excerpt presents his firsthand account and the ensuing controversy, showing how insider disclosures can catalyze public scrutiny and policy debate around surveillance. It underscores the lasting impact whistleblowers have on privacy advocacy and reform efforts.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>前 AT&amp;T 技术员 Mark Klein 向电子前哨基金会披露了“641A 室”的存在，据称在那里对光纤线路进行了大规模监控拦截。书摘给出他的第一手叙述及随后的争议，展示了内部披露如何点燃公众关注并推动 surveillance 相关的政策辩论。此事凸显举报人在隐私倡议与改革中的长期影响。</p>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-47909226' data-tags='Open Source,Hardware'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<button class='hn-digest-toggle' type='button' aria-expanded='false' title='Show summary and image' aria-label='Show summary and image'><span aria-hidden='true'>⌄</span></button>
<p class='hn-title'>(131) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://asahilinux.org/2026/04/progress-report-7-0/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Asahi Linux Progress Linux 7.0</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>Asahi Linux Progress Linux 7.0</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47909226' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 630</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47909226' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 323</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--green' data-tag='Open Source'>Open Source</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--slate' data-tag='Hardware'>Hardware</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Asahi Linux Progress Linux 7.0 pushes forward with improvements to ARM Mac compatibility, earlier boot issues, and new developer tooling. The project continues narrowing the gap between Apple Silicon hardware and open-source Linux.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>Asahi Linux Progress Linux 7.0 在 ARM Mac 兼容性、早期启动问题和开发工具方面取得改进，持续缩小 Apple Silicon 与开源 Linux 之间的差距。</p>

<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-47959463' data-tags='Privacy,Web'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<button class='hn-digest-toggle' type='button' aria-expanded='false' title='Show summary and image' aria-label='Show summary and image'><span aria-hidden='true'>⌄</span></button>
<p class='hn-title'>(132) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://github.com/mozilla/standards-positions/issues/1213#issuecomment-4347988313' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Mozilla&#x27;s opposition to Chrome&#x27;s Prompt API</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>Mozilla 反对 Chrome 的 Prompt API</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47959463' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 626</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47959463' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 224</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--red' data-tag='Privacy'>Privacy</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--cyan' data-tag='Web'>Web</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Mozilla has voiced concerns about Chrome&#x27;s Prompt API, arguing it could complicate user consent and privacy controls and fragment browser standards. The stance highlights ongoing tensions between platform features, privacy safeguards, and interoperability in browser ecosystems.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>Mozilla 对 Chrome 的 Prompt API 表态反对，担心会削弱用户同意与隐私控制，并破坏跨浏览器标准的一致性。这一立场凸显了浏览器生态中对隐私保护、功能设计与互操作性的持续博弈。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://opengraph.githubassets.com/051ef604bbf18878e0d34869e80cae4cb42b36e86956f6d67294e6faaf2b614a/mozilla/standards-positions/issues/1213' data-full='https://opengraph.githubassets.com/051ef604bbf18878e0d34869e80cae4cb42b36e86956f6d67294e6faaf2b614a/mozilla/standards-positions/issues/1213' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-47788385' data-tags='Programming,AI'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<button class='hn-digest-toggle' type='button' aria-expanded='false' title='Show summary and image' aria-label='Show summary and image'><span aria-hidden='true'>⌄</span></button>
<p class='hn-title'>(133) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://sleepingrobots.com/dreams/stop-using-ollama/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>The local LLM ecosystem doesn’t need Ollama</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>本地大模型生态不需要 Ollama</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47788385' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 626</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47788385' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 207</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--indigo' data-tag='Programming'>Programming</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--blue' data-tag='AI'>AI</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Ollama is not essential for local LLM workflows; the piece argues that the local ecosystem can be served by simpler tooling or native inference stacks, reducing friction and maintenance. It weighs trade-offs in updates, platform compatibility, and ecosystem maturity.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>本文认为本地大语言模型生态并不需要 Ollama；可以通过更简单的工具或原生推理栈完成同样任务，降低运维成本。文章比较了更新、平台兼容性和生态成熟度等权衡，指出 Ollama 可能并非必需。</p>

<div class='hn-body'>

<p class='hn-title'>(134) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://arhan.sh/blog/native-instant-space-switching-on-macos/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Native Instant Space Switching on macOS</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>macOS 原生即时空间切换</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47708818' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 626</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47708818' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 311</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--pink' data-tag='Design'>Design</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>macOS now supports native instant switching between virtual desktops (Spaces), drastically reducing the perceived latency when moving between spaces. It highlights implications for workflow efficiency, window management, and potential limitations compared with third-party tools.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>macOS 现在原生支持在虚拟桌面（Spaces）之间即时切换，显著降低切换时的延迟，提升多任务处理体验。文中讨论了这一特性对窗口管理和工作流程的影响，以及与第三方工具相比的潜在局限。</p>

<p class='hn-title'>(135) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://www.axios.com/2026/04/09/meta-social-media-addiction-ads' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Meta removes ads for social media addiction litigation</a></p>

<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47703419' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 626</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47703419' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 248</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--orange' data-tag='Legal'>Legal</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--amber' data-tag='Business'>Business</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Meta&#x27;s move to remove ads linked to a social-media addiction lawsuit underscores how litigation is nudging platforms to rethink ad-supported models and user-well-being commitments. The case could ripple through advertising strategies and policy discussions around platform responsibility.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>Meta 因社媒成瘾诉讼而移除相关广告，凸显法律诉讼正在推动平台重新审视广告支持模式与对用户健康的承诺。此举可能影响广告策略与对平台责任的政策讨论。</p>

<p class='hn-title'>(136) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://theonion.com/at-long-last-infowars-is-ours/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>At long last, InfoWars is ours</a></p>

<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47837611' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 624</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47837611' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 294</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--rose' data-tag='Culture'>Culture</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>A satirical piece imagines someone finally taking control of InfoWars, lampooning the power dynamics of fringe media and online info ecosystems. It skewers how ownership can shape narratives and the culture around conspiracy sites, offering a meta take on media influence.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>这篇讽刺文设想有人终于掌控 InfoWars，调侃边缘媒体的权力关系与信息生态。通过字句讽刺所有权如何塑造叙事，以及公众对阴谋论媒体的看法，折射出信息环境中的权力与影响。</p>

<p class='hn-title'>(137) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://ollama.com/blog/mlx' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Ollama is now powered by MLX on Apple Silicon in preview</a></p>

<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>Ollama 现已在 Apple Silicon 支持 MLX，提供本地推理的加速效果。预览版可能带来显著的性能提升和隐私优势，但仍处于早期阶段，需关注兼容性与稳定性。</p>

<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47637757' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 616</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47637757' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 185</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--blue' data-tag='AI'>AI</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>A simple self-distillation technique improves code generation quality in experiments, suggesting a cheap method to boost model performance without large compute overhead. It could be applied to training or inference of code assistants.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>研究显示一种极其简单的自蒸馏方法就能提升代码生成质量，成本低且易于在现有模型上实现。该方法对提升代码生成能力有潜在意义，可能为改进代码助手和编程相关任务提供可行路径。</p>

<div class='hn-body'>
<button class='hn-digest-toggle' type='button' aria-expanded='false' title='Show summary and image' aria-label='Show summary and image'><span aria-hidden='true'>⌄</span></button>
<p class='hn-title'>(139) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://cacm.acm.org/news/how-nasa-built-artemis-iis-fault-tolerant-computer/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>How NASA built Artemis II’s fault-tolerant computer</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>NASA 如何打造 Artemis II 的容错计算机</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47704804' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 616</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47704804' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 222</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--teal' data-tag='Space'>Space</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--slate' data-tag='Hardware'>Hardware</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>NASA built a fault-tolerant onboard computer for Artemis II, emphasizing resilience in radiation-rich space. The system relies on redundancy, error detection, and hardened components to keep crewed flight safe even if primary hardware fails. The design illustrates how space-grade computing priorities differ from consumer systems.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>阿耳忒弥斯II号任务的机载容错计算机采用冗余、错误检测和抗辐射组件等技术，以在关键硬件失效时仍能维持运作。该系统体现了太空计算对可靠性的高要求，远超商用系统的容错能力。</p>
</div>
</div>
</div>

<div class='hn-body'>

<p class='hn-title'>(140) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://www.jeffgeerling.com/blog/2026/dram-pricing-is-killing-the-hobbyist-sbc-market/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>DRAM pricing is killing the hobbyist SBC market</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>DRAM定价正在挤压爱好者级SBC市场</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47606840' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 614</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47606840' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 529</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--slate' data-tag='Hardware'>Hardware</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Rising DRAM prices are squeezing the hobbyist SBC market, where memory is a core cost. As memory becomes a larger share of BOM, many affordable boards struggle on margins and demand. The shift could push makers toward leaner designs or pre-bundled solutions.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>DRAM价格上涨挤压了爱好者级SBC市场，内存成本成为关键负担。随着内存占比上升，许多平价开发板的利润率和需求都被压缩。市场动荡可能促使厂商推出更节省内存的设计或打包解决方案。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://www.jeffgeerling.com/blog/2026/dram-pricing-is-killing-the-hobbyist-sbc-market/raspberry-pi-price-increase-since-launch.jpg' data-full='https://www.jeffgeerling.com/blog/2026/dram-pricing-is-killing-the-hobbyist-sbc-market/raspberry-pi-price-increase-since-launch.jpg' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>
