<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-47719486' data-tags='Politics,Open Source'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<button class='hn-digest-toggle' type='button' aria-expanded='false' title='Show summary and image' aria-label='Show summary and image'><span aria-hidden='true'>⌄</span></button>
<p class='hn-title'>(142) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://techcrunch.com/2026/04/10/france-to-ditch-windows-for-linux-to-reduce-reliance-on-us-tech/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>France to ditch Windows for Linux to reduce reliance on US tech</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>法国将放弃 Windows 改用 Linux，降低对美国科技的依赖</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47719486' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 612</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47719486' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 687</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--orange' data-tag='Politics'>Politics</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--green' data-tag='Open Source'>Open Source</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>France plans to replace Windows with Linux on government devices to reduce reliance on US tech and tighten sovereignty. The move signals growing appetite for open-source in public sector procurement and raises questions about migration complexity and vendor ecosystems.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>法国计划把政府设备从 Windows 切换到 Linux，以减少对美国科技的依赖。这一举措凸显公共部门对开源软件的越来越大兴趣，同时也带来迁移的挑战与生态影响。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://techcrunch.com/wp-content/uploads/2026/04/france-flag-2233577438.jpg?resize=1200,873' data-full='https://techcrunch.com/wp-content/uploads/2026/04/france-flag-2233577438.jpg?resize=1200,873' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-47672818' data-tags='Culture'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<button class='hn-digest-toggle' type='button' aria-expanded='false' title='Show summary and image' aria-label='Show summary and image'><span aria-hidden='true'>⌄</span></button>
<p class='hn-title'>(143) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://idiocracy.wtf/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Are We Idiocracy Yet?</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>我们真的走向智劣时代了吗？</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47672818' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 612</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47672818' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 534</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--rose' data-tag='Culture'>Culture</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>A provocative take on whether modern culture is drifting toward anti-intellectualism, highlighting tech-enabled misinformation and the ethics of online discourse.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>对现代文化是否走向反智主义的挑衅性观察，聚焦科技驱动的信息误导以及网络话语的伦理问题。</p>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-47677853' data-tags='AI'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<button class='hn-digest-toggle' type='button' aria-expanded='false' title='Show summary and image' aria-label='Show summary and image'><span aria-hidden='true'>⌄</span></button>
<p class='hn-title'>(144) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://z.ai/blog/glm-5.1' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>GLM-5.1: Towards Long-Horizon Tasks</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>GLM-5.1：迈向长时域任务</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47677853' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 611</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47677853' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 255</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--blue' data-tag='AI'>AI</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>GLM-5.1 advances long-horizon reasoning, aiming to improve task planning and sustained context handling in large language models. The update signals continued the trend toward more capable multi-step problem solving, useful for complex workflows.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>GLM-5.1 在长期推理能力上取得进展，提升任务规划与长上下文处理。该更新延续了让大型语言模型进行多步推理、处理复杂任务的趋势，对需要跨长文档的应用尤为有用。</p>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-47816960' data-tags='AI,Programming'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<button class='hn-digest-toggle' type='button' aria-expanded='false' title='Show summary and image' aria-label='Show summary and image'><span aria-hidden='true'>⌄</span></button>
<p class='hn-title'>(145) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://tokens.billchambers.me/leaderboard' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Anonymous request-token comparisons from Opus 4.6 and Opus 4.7</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>Opus 4.6 与 Opus 4.7 的匿名请求令牌比较</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47816960' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 610</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47816960' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 571</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--blue' data-tag='AI'>AI</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--indigo' data-tag='Programming'>Programming</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>A public leaderboard contrasts how many tokens are consumed by anonymous requests across Opus 4.6 and Opus 4.7. The results illustrate how a version bump can change token efficiency, with practical implications for cost-conscious API users and performance tuning.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>公开的排行榜对比 Opus 4.6 与 4.7 在匿名请求中消耗的令牌数量，揭示版本迭代对 token 效率的影响。对希望控制成本和优化性能的开发者来说，这类比较提供了有价值的基准。</p>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-47664912' data-tags='Culture,Programming'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<button class='hn-digest-toggle' type='button' aria-expanded='false' title='Show summary and image' aria-label='Show summary and image'><span aria-hidden='true'>⌄</span></button>
<p class='hn-title'>(146) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://bramcohen.com/p/the-cult-of-vibe-coding-is-insane' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>The cult of vibe coding is dogfooding run amok</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>氛围编码的崇拜：自我喂养文化失控</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47664912' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 609</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47664912' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 505</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--rose' data-tag='Culture'>Culture</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--indigo' data-tag='Programming'>Programming</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>The piece argues that &#x27;vibe coding&#x27;—prioritizing aesthetics, culture, and vibes over engineering rigor—has become a dogfooding trap, where teams use internal satisfaction as a proxy for quality. It warns such culture can erode product reliability, real user outcomes, and long-run sustainability. The takeaway is to balance vibe with substance and user needs.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>本文批评了所谓的“氛围编码”现象，强调注重美学与氛围而忽视工程严谨的趋势成为自我喂养的陷阱。内部测试和满足感被当成质量指标，可能侵蚀产品可靠性、真实用户价值与长期可持续性。核心是要在氛围和实质、用户需求之间取得平衡。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://substackcdn.com/image/fetch/$s_!93xj!,f_auto,q_auto:best,fl_progressive:steep/https%3A%2F%2Fbramcohen.substack.com%2Ftwitter%2Fsubscribe-card.jpg%3Fv%3D-1733548744%26version%3D9' data-full='https://substackcdn.com/image/fetch/$s_!93xj!,f_auto,q_auto:best,fl_progressive:steep/https%3A%2F%2Fbramcohen.substack.com%2Ftwitter%2Fsubscribe-card.jpg%3Fv%3D-1733548744%26version%3D9' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>

<p class='hn-title'>(147) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='http://www.terrybisson.com/theyre-made-out-of-meat-2/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>They&#x27;re made out of meat (1991)</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>它们都是肉做的（1991）</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47688678' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 602</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47688678' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 161</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--rose' data-tag='Culture'>Culture</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Aliens discover humans are meat and struggle to understand Earth&#x27;s beings; the story uses a stark premise to question assumptions about life, intelligence, and cross-cultural communication. It remains a sharp, classic piece on perspective.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>外星人发现人类其实是肉体所构成，他们努力理解地球生命与意识的边界。通过简练又讽刺的情节，质疑关于生命、智能与跨文化沟通的假设。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='http://www.terrybisson.com/wp-content/uploads/2019/04/TB-logo-2.png' data-full='http://www.terrybisson.com/wp-content/uploads/2019/04/TB-logo-2.png' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>

<div class='hn-body'>
<button class='hn-digest-toggle' type='button' aria-expanded='false' title='Show summary and image' aria-label='Show summary and image'><span aria-hidden='true'>⌄</span></button>
<p class='hn-title'>(148) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://www.theguardian.com/world/2026/apr/03/us-fighter-jet-confirmed-shot-down-over-iran' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>F-15E jet shot down over Iran</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>F-15E 战斗机在伊朗上空被击落</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47628326' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 601</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47628326' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 1365</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--red' data-tag='Security'>Security</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--orange' data-tag='Politics'>Politics</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>An F-15E was shot down over Iran, marking a significant escalation in regional tensions. The incident underscores the fragility of aviation assets in volatile zones and the risk of broader security spillovers. It will likely influence defense Posture and policy considerations.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>一架 F-15E 战斗机在伊朗上空被击落，标志着区域紧张局势的升级。此事件凸显在高风险地区维护航空资产的脆弱性与潜在的安全扩散风险，可能影响防务态势与政策取向。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://i.guim.co.uk/img/media/482982697628386efabd0a787d9590da0690e8a1/0_0_5000_4000/master/5000.jpg?width=1200&amp;height=630&amp;quality=85&amp;auto=format&amp;fit=crop&amp;precrop=40:21,offset-x50,offset-y0&amp;overlay-align=bottom%2Cleft&amp;overlay-width=100p&amp;overlay-base64=L2ltZy9zdGF0aWMvb3ZlcmxheXMvdGctZGVmYXVsdC5wbmc&amp;enable=upscale&amp;s=71aa55243556fe4276e3975782e4c1c6' data-full='https://i.guim.co.uk/img/media/482982697628386efabd0a787d9590da0690e8a1/0_0_5000_4000/master/5000.jpg?width=1200&amp;height=630&amp;quality=85&amp;auto=format&amp;fit=crop&amp;precrop=40:21,offset-x50,offset-y0&amp;overlay-align=bottom%2Cleft&amp;overlay-width=100p&amp;overlay-base64=L2ltZy9zdGF0aWMvb3ZlcmxheXMvdGctZGVmYXVsdC5wbmc&amp;enable=upscale&amp;s=71aa55243556fe4276e3975782e4c1c6' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>

<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-47658146' data-tags='Finance,Web'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<button class='hn-digest-toggle' type='button' aria-expanded='false' title='Show summary and image' aria-label='Show summary and image'><span aria-hidden='true'>⌄</span></button>
<p class='hn-title'>(149) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://www.mining.com/france-pulls-last-gold-held-in-us-for-15b-gain/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>France pulls last gold held in US</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>法国从美国取回最后一批黄金，获利约150亿美元</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47658146' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 600</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47658146' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 334</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--amber' data-tag='Finance'>Finance</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--cyan' data-tag='Web'>Web</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>France moved its remaining gold reserves from the United States, realizing about $15 billion in gains. The move underscores ongoing central-bank diversification away from custody risk and signals geopolitical considerations in asset custody. It also highlights how precious metals markets react to sovereign reserve shifts.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>法国将其在美黄金储备中的最后一部分取回，实现约150亿美元的收益。这显示央行在分散储备时继续降低对单一托管方的依赖，并反映了资产保管的地缘政治考量。除此之外，此类举动往往会影响贵金属市场的供需和价格走向。</p>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-47689648' data-tags='AI'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<button class='hn-digest-toggle' type='button' aria-expanded='false' title='Show summary and image' aria-label='Show summary and image'><span aria-hidden='true'>⌄</span></button>
<p class='hn-title'>(150) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://aphyr.com/posts/411-the-future-of-everything-is-lies-i-guess' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>ML promises to be profoundly weird</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>机器学习将带来极其怪异的未来</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47689648' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 598</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47689648' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 591</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--blue' data-tag='AI'>AI</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Argues that ML systems will exhibit unusual, sometimes counterintuitive behavior as data and objectives shift, complicating testing and reliability. The piece reflects on implications for software engineering, governance, and trust in AI.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>指出机器学习系统在数据分布和目标变化下会呈现异常甚至反直觉的行为，这会让测试与可靠性变得复杂。讨论对软件工程、治理与对 AI 信任的影响。</p>

<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-47682276' data-tags='Politics,Security'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<button class='hn-digest-toggle' type='button' aria-expanded='false' title='Show summary and image' aria-label='Show summary and image'><span aria-hidden='true'>⌄</span></button>
<p class='hn-title'>(151) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://www.theguardian.com/us-news/2026/apr/07/trump-iran-war-ceasefire' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>US and Iran agree to provisional ceasefire</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>美伊就临时停火达成一致</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47682276' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 594</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47682276' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 1980</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--orange' data-tag='Politics'>Politics</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--red' data-tag='Security'>Security</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>After talks, the US and Iran reach a provisional ceasefire, signaling a de-escalation step amid ongoing tensions. The arrangement creates space for negotiations and potential humanitarian corridors, though fragility remains.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>经谈判，美伊就临时停火达成协议，表明紧张局势有所缓和。协议为继续谈判和人道主义走廊争取时间，但局势仍不稳固。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://i.guim.co.uk/img/media/7cde4a66207fd4d8c625b447df29716f6e1a7235/576_0_4582_3666/master/4582.jpg?width=1200&amp;height=630&amp;quality=85&amp;auto=format&amp;fit=crop&amp;precrop=40:21,offset-x50,offset-y0&amp;overlay-align=bottom%2Cleft&amp;overlay-width=100p&amp;overlay-base64=L2ltZy9zdGF0aWMvb3ZlcmxheXMvdGctZGVmYXVsdC5wbmc&amp;enable=upscale&amp;s=b2a8729bf6c3d7f318035c7c52fbec3a' data-full='https://i.guim.co.uk/img/media/7cde4a66207fd4d8c625b447df29716f6e1a7235/576_0_4582_3666/master/4582.jpg?width=1200&amp;height=630&amp;quality=85&amp;auto=format&amp;fit=crop&amp;precrop=40:21,offset-x50,offset-y0&amp;overlay-align=bottom%2Cleft&amp;overlay-width=100p&amp;overlay-base64=L2ltZy9zdGF0aWMvb3ZlcmxheXMvdGctZGVmYXVsdC5wbmc&amp;enable=upscale&amp;s=b2a8729bf6c3d7f318035c7c52fbec3a' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>

<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-47716490' data-tags='Security,Privacy'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<button class='hn-digest-toggle' type='button' aria-expanded='false' title='Show summary and image' aria-label='Show summary and image'><span aria-hidden='true'>⌄</span></button>
<p class='hn-title'>(152) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://9to5mac.com/2026/04/09/fbi-used-iphone-notification-data-to-retrieve-deleted-signal-messages/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>FBI used iPhone notification data to retrieve deleted Signal messages</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>FBI 使用 iPhone 通知数据检索已删除的 Signal 消息</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47716490' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 593</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47716490' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 293</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--red' data-tag='Security'>Security</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--red' data-tag='Privacy'>Privacy</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Forensic use of iPhone notification data enabled recovery of deleted Signal messages, highlighting how notification metadata can bypass user deletions. This underscores a growing privacy and security risk for messaging apps, and could intensify debates over data minimization and platform protections.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>通过分析 iPhone 通知数据，联邦调查局帮助找回了已删除的 Signal 消息，暴露了通知元数据可能绕过删除保护的风险。此事凸显隐私与安全之间的博弈，或将推动关于数据最小化与平台保护的讨论。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://i0.wp.com/9to5mac.com/wp-content/uploads/sites/6/2025/12/Reddit-comment-led-police-to-identify-Brown-University-shooter.jpg?resize=1200%2C628&amp;quality=82&amp;strip=all&amp;ssl=1' data-full='https://i0.wp.com/9to5mac.com/wp-content/uploads/sites/6/2025/12/Reddit-comment-led-police-to-identify-Brown-University-shooter.jpg?resize=1200%2C628&amp;quality=82&amp;strip=all&amp;ssl=1' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-47948603' data-tags='Open Source,Politics'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<button class='hn-digest-toggle' type='button' aria-expanded='false' title='Show summary and image' aria-label='Show summary and image'><span aria-hidden='true'>⌄</span></button>
<p class='hn-title'>(153) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://blog.tangled.org/federation/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>We need a federation of forges</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>Tangled：我们需要一个联邦制的代码托管平台网络</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47948603' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 592</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47948603' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 388</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--green' data-tag='Open Source'>Open Source</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--orange' data-tag='Politics'>Politics</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Tangled argues for a federation of code forges to avoid vendor lock-in and improve collaboration. It sketches a model for interoperable identities, permissions, and data exchange across platforms, with governance considerations for a healthy ecosystem.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>Tangled 主张建立一个联邦制的代码托管平台网络，以避免厂商锁定并提升协作。文中勾画了在各个平台间实现互操作身份、权限与数据交换的模型，并就健康生态的治理提出思路。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://assets.tangled.network/blog/federation/Light-OG.png' data-full='https://assets.tangled.network/blog/federation/Light-OG.png' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>

<div class='hn-body'>
<button class='hn-digest-toggle' type='button' aria-expanded='false' title='Show summary and image' aria-label='Show summary and image'><span aria-hidden='true'>⌄</span></button>
<p class='hn-title'>(154) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://simonomi.dev/blog/color-code-your-bytes/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Your hex editor should color-code bytes</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>你的十六进制编辑器应对字节进行颜色编码</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47846688' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 589</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47846688' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 153</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--indigo' data-tag='Programming'>Programming</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>A post argues color-coding bytes in hex editors improves readability and reduces mistakes when inspecting binary data. It discusses schemes for distinguishing text vs binary regions, endianness, and pattern detection.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>文章主张在十六进制编辑器中对字节进行颜色编码，以提升可读性并降低读错的风险。它提出将 ASCII 区域、二进制数据、常见魔数等用不同颜色标示，以及如何辅助识别模式和字节序等细节。</p>

<div class='hn-body'>

<p class='hn-title'>(155) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://www.jeffgeerling.com/blog/2026/new-10-gbe-usb-adapters-cooler-smaller-cheaper/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>New 10 GbE USB adapters are cooler, smaller, cheaper</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>新款 10 GbE USB 适配器更酷更小更便宜</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47899053' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 588</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47899053' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 349</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--slate' data-tag='Hardware'>Hardware</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>New 10GbE USB adapters bring more affordable, compact, and practical options for high-speed network expansion. They lower the barrier for homelabs, media servers, and USB-C powered devices to add fast Ethernet without PCIe NICs. Consider driver support and power draw when choosing a model.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>新款 10GbE USB 适配器体积更小、价格更低，降低了在家用实验室、媒体服务器等场景扩展高端网络的门槛。无需 PCIe NIC 就能实现千兆级别的以太网速率，适合搭配 USB-C 设备使用。选购时留意驱动支持和供电需求。</p>

</div>
</div>

<p class='hn-title'>(156) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://www.hypertalking.com/2023/05/08/1-bit-pixel-art-of-hokusais-the-great-wave-off-kanagawa/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>1-Bit Hokusai&#x27;s &quot;The Great Wave&quot; (2023)</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>1 位元像素風格的《神奈川沖浪裏》（2023）</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47863570' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 588</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47863570' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 90</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--purple' data-tag='Entertainment'>Entertainment</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--rose' data-tag='Culture'>Culture</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>A 1-bit pixel-art reinterpretation of Hokusai&#x27;s The Great Wave Off Kanagawa, created in 2023. The piece demonstrates how ultra-minimal digital art can echo a masterwork&#x27;s motion and drama, pushing the boundaries of what &#x27;low fidelity&#x27; can convey. It underscores the enduring appeal of classic images in the era of retro aesthetics and generative art.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>用1位元像素風格重新詮釋葛飾北斎的《神奈川沖浪裏》，作品於2023年亮相。極簡像素的限制放大波濤的動勢與張力，顯示經典名作在現代數位美學中的再創造力與吸引力。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://www.hypertalking.com/wp-content/themes/hypertalking/images/james-colour.png' data-full='https://www.hypertalking.com/wp-content/themes/hypertalking/images/james-colour.png' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-47698455' data-tags='Gaming,Web'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<button class='hn-digest-toggle' type='button' aria-expanded='false' title='Show summary and image' aria-label='Show summary and image'><span aria-hidden='true'>⌄</span></button>
<p class='hn-title'>(157) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://playstarfling.com' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Starfling: A one-tap endless orbital slingshot game in a single HTML file</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>Starfling：一个 HTML 单文件的无尽轨道弹射游戏</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47698455' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 585</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47698455' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 146</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--purple' data-tag='Gaming'>Gaming</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--cyan' data-tag='Web'>Web</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Starfling is a minimalist browser game playable in a single HTML file, using a tap to control orbital slingshots. It showcases how far a tiny, self-contained web app can push interactivity and visuals with pure HTML/JS, without a build or asset pipeline. A fun example of ultra-lightweight game development and portability.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>Starfling 是一个极简的浏览器游戏，直接在单个 HTML 文件中就能运行，点击一次即可进行轨道弹射。它演示了使用纯 HTML/JS 就能实现丰富交互与流畅画面的极致轻量化游戏，并且无需构建工具即可带来完整体验。</p>
</div>
</div>
</div>

<div class='hn-body'>

<p class='hn-title'>(158) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://letsdatascience.com/news/atlassian-enables-default-data-collection-to-train-ai-f71343d8' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Atlassian enables default data collection to train AI</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>Atlassian 启用默认数据收集以训练 AI</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47833247' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 582</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47833247' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 130</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--blue' data-tag='AI'>AI</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--red' data-tag='Privacy'>Privacy</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Atlassian plans to enable default data collection to train AI across its product suite, accelerating model improvements through user interactions. The move boosts AI-powered features but raises privacy, data governance, and policy questions for enterprises.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>Atlassian 计划在其产品套件中默认收集数据用于训练 AI，从而通过用户交互来加速模型改进。这提升了 AI 驱动的功能，但也引发隐私、数据治理与企业政策方面的担忧。</p>
</div>
</div>
</div>

<p class='hn-title'>(159) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://www.404media.co/microsoft-abruptly-terminates-veracrypt-account-halting-windows-updates/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Microsoft terminates VeraCrypt account, halting Windows updates</a></p>

<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Reports indicate Microsoft terminated a VeraCrypt account, halting Windows updates. The incident underscores how dependence on external accounts can disrupt critical software maintenance and raises questions about vendor policies.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>报道称微软突然终止了VeraCrypt账户，导致Windows更新中断。这一事件凸显对外部账户的依赖如何影响关键软件维护，并引发对厂商政策的关注。</p>

<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>该视频用家用组件演示一个 RAM 原型，帮助理解内存如何工作。它讲解存储单元、时序，以及在家自制 RAM 相关电路所遇到的挑战，直观展现了内存架构的要点。</p>

<div class='hn-body'>

<p class='hn-title'>(162) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://app.oravys.com/blog/mercor-breach-2026' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>4TB of voice samples just stolen from 40k AI contractors at Mercor</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>Mercor 的外包承包商数据泄露：4 TB 语音样本被窃取</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47919630' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 578</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47919630' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 218</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--red' data-tag='Privacy'>Privacy</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--red' data-tag='Security'>Security</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--blue' data-tag='AI'>AI</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Mercor suffered a breach exposing 4TB of voice samples from about 40,000 AI contractors. The incident underscores the privacy and security risks in outsourced data-labeling workflows and could fuel debates about consent, governance, and stronger data handling controls.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>Mercor 的安全事件暴露了来自约4万名 AI 合作者的4 TB 语音样本。此事凸显外包数据标注流程中的隐私与安全风险，或促使对数据授权、治理与更强数据处理控制的讨论。</p>

</div>
</div>
</div>

<p class='hn-title'>(163) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://www.skoda-storyboard.com/en/skoda-world/skoda-duobell-a-bicycle-bell-that-outsmarts-even-smart-headphones/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Škoda DuoBell: A bicycle bell that penetrates noise-cancelling headphones</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>Škoda DuoBell：一枚能穿透降噪耳机的自行车铃</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47687248' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 574</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47687248' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 572</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--slate' data-tag='Hardware'>Hardware</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--pink' data-tag='Design'>Design</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Škoda&#x27;s DuoBell uses an audible signal designed to cut through active noise cancellation, ensuring cyclists are noticed. It highlights practical hardware design for urban mobility and how audible cues interact with consumer audio tech.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>Škoda 的 DuoBell 设计成在主动降噪耳机下仍能发出清晰声音，确保骑行者被听见。该产品凸显城市出行的实用硬件设计，以及可听信号与消费级音频技术的互动。</p>

<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-47733217' data-tags='AI'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<button class='hn-digest-toggle' type='button' aria-expanded='false' title='Show summary and image' aria-label='Show summary and image'><span aria-hidden='true'>⌄</span></button>
<p class='hn-title'>(164) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://rdi.berkeley.edu/blog/trustworthy-benchmarks-cont/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Exploiting the most prominent AI agent benchmarks</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>利用最显著的 AI 代理基准测试</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47733217' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 573</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47733217' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 137</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--blue' data-tag='AI'>AI</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Berkeley piece critiques how prominent AI benchmarks are exploited or gamed, questioning reliability of current benchmarks for real-world agent evaluation. It argues for more robust, trustworthy benchmarks to guide research and product decisions.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>Berkeley 的文章质疑当前最显著的 AI 代理基准的健壮性，指出评测结果可能被操纵或不充分反映现实场景。它呼吁建立更可信的基准，以正确引导研究方向和产品决策。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://rdi.berkeley.edu/assets/images/rdi_logo_horizontal_720.png' data-full='https://rdi.berkeley.edu/assets/images/rdi_logo_horizontal_720.png' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-47587866' data-tags='AI,Legal'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<button class='hn-digest-toggle' type='button' aria-expanded='false' title='Show summary and image' aria-label='Show summary and image'><span aria-hidden='true'>⌄</span></button>
<p class='hn-title'>(165) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://www.microsoft.com/en-us/microsoft-copilot/for-individuals/termsofuse' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Microsoft: Copilot is for entertainment purposes only</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>微软：Copilot 仅用于娱乐用途</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47587866' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 571</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47587866' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 199</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--blue' data-tag='AI'>AI</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--orange' data-tag='Legal'>Legal</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Microsoft explicitly states in its terms that Copilot is for entertainment purposes only, clarifying its intended use and limitations. This stance raises questions about relying on AI for critical coding or decision-making and highlights the need for clear risk disclosures.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>微软在使用条款中明确表示 Copilot 仅用于娱乐用途，界定了其使用范围与局限。此说法引发了人们对将其用于关键编码或决策场景的可依赖性以及需披露的风险信息的关注。</p>

<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-47806096' data-tags='Culture,Hardware'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<button class='hn-digest-toggle' type='button' aria-expanded='false' title='Show summary and image' aria-label='Show summary and image'><span aria-hidden='true'>⌄</span></button>
<p class='hn-title'>(166) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://archive.org/details/byte-magazine-1975-09' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Archive of BYTE magazine, starting with issue #1 in 1975</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>Byte 杂志档案，起始于1975年第1期</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47806096' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 569</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47806096' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 147</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--rose' data-tag='Culture'>Culture</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--slate' data-tag='Hardware'>Hardware</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Archive of Byte magazine from 1975 onward provides a window into early microcomputer culture. It collects hardware hacks, software reviews, and stories that shaped early personal computing. For historians and tinkerers, it&#x27;s a valuable snapshot of the era&#x27;s ideas and constraints.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>Byte 杂志档案为人们打开了1970年代末至1980年代初计算机文化的窗口。它汇集了硬件 hacks、软件评测以及塑造早期个人计算的故事。对于历史研究者和动手爱好者来说，这是了解那个时代思想与局限性的宝贵快照。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://archive.org/services/img/byte-magazine-1975-09' data-full='https://archive.org/services/img/byte-magazine-1975-09' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>

<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Adam Back&#x27;s profile in the New York Times traces attempts to uncover Satoshi Nakamoto&#x27;s true identity, weighing rumored candidates and the evidentiary curve.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>纽约时报刊文回顾 Adam Back 的追踪，试图揭示中本聪的真实身份，评估候选人及相关证据。报道揭示了这场追寻如何交织密码学史、隐私议题，以及比特币创始人神话的形成。</p>

<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>提出每天一个点的管理体系以对抗数字杂乱，强调通过小而持续的行动来提升组织与专注度。低摩擦的习惯可以逐步带来长期效果。</p>

<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Tailscale unveils a new macOS app called Home, designed to streamline onboarding and network access on Macs. The release focuses on a native-like experience and features aimed at improving usability for Mac users, including improvements around how the app accommodates display quirks like the notch.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>Tailscale 推出名为 Home 的 macOS 应用，旨在简化 Mac 的网络接入与初始配置。新应用强调原生化体验，并通过改进对显示凹槽等硬件细节的适配，提升 Mac 用户的使用便捷性。</p>

<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Germany&#x27;s eIDAS rollout may hinge on Apple/Google account authentication, raising privacy and control questions for digital identity systems. The piece analyzes architectural trade-offs and user impact.</p>

<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>A user reports a mystery app that installs itself daily on iPhone, prompting questions about how apps gain such persistence and what privacy controls failed. <p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>用户报告每天在 iPhone 上悄悄新增安装的应用，引发关于应用如何获得持续权限与隐私控制失效的质疑。讨论聚焦 iOS 安全边界的薄弱点，呼吁加强应用审核和用户保护。</p>

<p class='hn-title'>(173) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://updates.thunderbird.net/en-US/thunderbird/140.0/apr26-1e/donate/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Help Keep Thunderbird Alive</a></p>

<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Thunderbird, the open-source email client, is running a fundraising drive to stay active. The appeal underscores ongoing challenges for volunteer-driven projects in sustaining development and support, even as mainstream clients rise. Donating helps ensure continued updates, privacy features, and localization support for users who value control over their data.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>开源邮件客户端 Thunderbird 正发起募捐以维持项目运营。社区呼吁捐助以解决长期资金与维护挑战，确保未来仍具备更新、隐私功能及本地化支持。</p>

<div class='hn-body'>
<button class='hn-digest-toggle' type='button' aria-expanded='false' title='Show summary and image' aria-label='Show summary and image'><span aria-hidden='true'>⌄</span></button>
<p class='hn-title'>(174) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://www.derekthompson.org/p/if-americas-so-rich-howd-it-get-so' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>If America&#x27;s so rich, how&#x27;d it get so sad?</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>美国这么富有，怎会变得如此悲伤？</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47877429' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 546</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47877429' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 1056</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--orange' data-tag='Politics'>Politics</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--amber' data-tag='Business'>Business</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--blue' data-tag='AI'>AI</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Derek Thompson argues that America&#x27;s wealth hasn&#x27;t translated into happiness due to inequality, social fragmentation, and policy choices; the piece connects economic growth with wellbeing and the impact of technology like AI.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>德里克·汤普森认为美国的繁荣并未带来相应的幸福感，原因在于不平等、社会分化和政策选择。文章探讨经济增长、社会福祉之间的错配，以及技术（包括 AI）对这种关系的影响。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://substackcdn.com/image/fetch/$s_!gAKe!,w_1200,h_675,c_fill,f_jpg,q_auto:good,fl_progressive:steep,g_auto/https%3A%2F%2Fsubstack-post-media.s3.amazonaws.com%2Fpublic%2Fimages%2F16200edd-4e29-4729-8d58-4eefbab120d8_1260x893.png' data-full='https://substackcdn.com/image/fetch/$s_!gAKe!,w_1200,h_675,c_fill,f_jpg,q_auto:good,fl_progressive:steep,g_auto/https%3A%2F%2Fsubstack-post-media.s3.amazonaws.com%2Fpublic%2Fimages%2F16200edd-4e29-4729-8d58-4eefbab120d8_1260x893.png' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-47945918' data-tags='Open Source,Security,Politics'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<button class='hn-digest-toggle' type='button' aria-expanded='false' title='Show summary and image' aria-label='Show summary and image'><span aria-hidden='true'>⌄</span></button>
<p class='hn-title'>(175) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://www.nldigitalgovernment.nl/news/soft-launch-for-government-open-source-code-platform/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Soft launch of open-source code platform for government</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>政府开放源代码平台进行软启动</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47945918' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 545</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47945918' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 124</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--green' data-tag='Open Source'>Open Source</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--red' data-tag='Security'>Security</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--orange' data-tag='Politics'>Politics</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>A government-backed open-source code platform is entering a soft launch, inviting developers to contribute and test components for public-sector software. The initiative aims to accelerate modernization, improve security through collaborative development, and reduce vendor lock-in.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>由政府牵头的开源代码平台进入软启动阶段，邀请开发者参与测试和贡献。此举旨在加速公共部门软件现代化、提升安全性并减少厂商锁定。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://www.nldigitalgovernment.nl/wp-content/uploads/sites/11/2026/04/pexels-mikhail-nilov-7988761.jpg' data-full='https://www.nldigitalgovernment.nl/wp-content/uploads/sites/11/2026/04/pexels-mikhail-nilov-7988761.jpg' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-47965108' data-tags='Security'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<button class='hn-digest-toggle' type='button' aria-expanded='false' title='Show summary and image' aria-label='Show summary and image'><span aria-hidden='true'>⌄</span></button>
<p class='hn-title'>(176) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://www.openwall.com/lists/oss-security/2026/04/30/10' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>For Linux kernel vulnerabilities, there is no heads-up to distributions</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>Linux 内核漏洞：对发行版没有事先通知</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47965108' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 542</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47965108' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 441</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--red' data-tag='Security'>Security</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Linux kernel vulnerability disclosures often fail to provide a formal heads-up to distributions, delaying patch adoption. The situation highlights gaps in how advisories propagate from researchers to downstream users, affecting enterprise risk management.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>Linux 内核漏洞的披露往往没有直接向发行版提供正式的事先通知，导致修补落地延迟。这暴露了研究人员、公告渠道与发行版维护之间的信息传递断层，影响企业的风险管理和修补节奏。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://www.openwall.com/logo.png' data-full='https://www.openwall.com/logo.png' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>

<div class='hn-body'>

<p class='hn-title'>(177) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://lists.zx2c4.com/pipermail/wireguard/2026-April/009561.html' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>WireGuard makes new Windows release following Microsoft signing resolution</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>WireGuard 在微软签名决定后推出全新 Windows 版本</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47719942' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 541</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47719942' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 162</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--green' data-tag='Open Source'>Open Source</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--red' data-tag='Security'>Security</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>WireGuard released a Windows build aligned with a Microsoft signing resolution, addressing long-standing driver-signing hurdles. The update improves installer reliability and kernel-mode VPN performance, underscoring how platform signing policies shape open-source tooling on Windows.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>在微软签名决议推动下，WireGuard 推出新的 Windows 版本，解决长期的驱动签名问题。更新提升安装可靠性与内核模式下的性能，体现了平台签名策略对开源工具在 Windows 上的影响。</p>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-47662234' data-tags='Science,Security'>
<div class='hn-body'>

<p class='hn-title'>(178) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://words.filippo.io/crqc-timeline/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>A cryptography engineer&#x27;s perspective on quantum computing timelines</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>量子计算时间线：一位密码学工程师的观点</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47662234' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 541</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47662234' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 242</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--teal' data-tag='Science'>Science</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--red' data-tag='Security'>Security</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>A cryptography engineer offers a grounded view on quantum timelines, arguing that practical quantum attacks require far more qubits and error correction than currently available. The piece stresses adopting post-quantum cryptography now and planning risk mitigation as a prudent long-term strategy.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>一位密码学工程师对量子计算的时间线给出务实的看法，认为要实现实用的量子攻击仍需远多于现有的量子比特与纠错能力。文章强调应尽早采用后量子密码并制定长期的风险缓解计划。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://assets.buttondown.email/images/e29aa9d6-cb9e-40ee-9340-a2d128ddaabf.jpeg?w=960&amp;fit=max' data-full='https://assets.buttondown.email/images/e29aa9d6-cb9e-40ee-9340-a2d128ddaabf.jpeg?w=960&amp;fit=max' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>

<div class='hn-body'>

<p class='hn-title'>(179) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://www.eff.org/deeplinks/2026/04/faas-temporary-flight-restriction-drones-blatant-attempt-criminalize-filming-ice' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>The FAA’s flight restriction for drones is an attempt to criminalize filming ICE</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>FAA 的无人机飞行限制试图将拍摄 ICE 刑事化</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47633947' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 536</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47633947' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 181</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--orange' data-tag='Politics'>Politics</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--orange' data-tag='Legal'>Legal</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>The FAA&#x27;s temporary drone flight restriction near certain facilities has drawn criticism for appearing to criminalize filming ICE activities and limiting First Amendment coverage of law enforcement.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>针对特定区域的无人机飞行限制引发担忧，批评者认为这等举措将拍摄 ICE 的行为刑事化并削弱对执法活动的监督报道。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://www.eff.org/files/banner_library/drone-happy.png' data-full='https://www.eff.org/files/banner_library/drone-happy.png' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-47612724' data-tags='AI,Open Source,Hardware'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<button class='hn-digest-toggle' type='button' aria-expanded='false' title='Show summary and image' aria-label='Show summary and image'><span aria-hidden='true'>⌄</span></button>
<p class='hn-title'>(180) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://lemonade-server.ai' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Lemonade by AMD: a fast and open source local LLM server using GPU and NPU</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>AMD 的 Lemonade：一个使用 GPU/NPU 的快速开源本地 LLM 服务器</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47612724' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 535</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47612724' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 111</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--blue' data-tag='AI'>AI</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--green' data-tag='Open Source'>Open Source</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--slate' data-tag='Hardware'>Hardware</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>AMD&#x27;s Lemonade project showcases a fast, open-source local LLM server that uses both GPUs and NPUs to run offline, private inference. By removing cloud dependencies, it aims to empower private AI workloads on consumer hardware, with implications for data control and customization.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>AMD 的 Lemonade 项目是一个快速、开源的本地大语言模型服务器，利用 GPU 和 NPU 实现离线私有推理。它让在本地硬件上运行强大模型成为可能，提升数据隐私与定制化能力。</p>

</div>
</div>
</div>

<div class='hn-body'>

<p class='hn-title'>(181) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://flipdisc.io' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Flipdiscs</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>Flipdiscs：翻转圆盘</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47875795' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 534</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47875795' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 85</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--purple' data-tag='Gaming'>Gaming</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--pink' data-tag='Design'>Design</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--blue' data-tag='AI'>AI</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Introducing Flipdiscs, a minimalist flipping-disk puzzle that challenges strategy with simple rules. The post delves into core mechanics, potential AI challenges, and how small rule sets can yield deep, emergent gameplay. It’s a case study in design restraint and replayability.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>介绍了 Flipdiscs 这款极简翻转圆盘的拼图游戏，靠简单规则考验策略。文章探讨了核心机制、潜在的 AI 挑战，以及如何通过小规则集实现深度和高可玩性，成为设计克制与可重玩性的案例。</p>

<p class='hn-title'>(182) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://www.wesnoth.org' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Battle for Wesnoth: open-source, turn-based strategy game</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>Wesnoth：开源的回合制策略游戏</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47664186' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 534</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47664186' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 161</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--green' data-tag='Open Source'>Open Source</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--purple' data-tag='Gaming'>Gaming</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Wesnoth is a long-running open-source turn-based strategy game with a fantasy setting and a large catalog of campaigns. The project demonstrates how community-driven development can sustain a complex strategy title, with robust modding support and cross-platform availability. Its enduring viability highlights the strength of open-source ecosystems for niche, mechanics-heavy games.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>Wesnoth 是一款长期由社区维护的开源回合制策略游戏，拥有丰富的战役和多人对战。项目展示了开源社区如何支撑复杂策略游戏的持续开发，以及跨平台和可扩展的模组生态。它的长期活力凸显了开源生态在维护高复杂性游戏中的作用。</p>

<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>jj is a new command-line interface for Jujutsu, aiming to simplify interaction with the Jujutsu framework. It provides a convenient, scriptable way to perform core tasks and experiments, reflecting a broader trend toward CLI-first tooling in niche systems.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>jj 是一个新的 Jujutsu 命令行界面，旨在简化与 Jujutsu 框架的交互。它提供一个便捷、可脚本化的方式来执行核心任务和实验，体现了在小众系统中向 CLI 优先工具的总体趋势。</p>

<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-47769089' data-tags='Security'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<button class='hn-digest-toggle' type='button' aria-expanded='false' title='Show summary and image' aria-label='Show summary and image'><span aria-hidden='true'>⌄</span></button>
<p class='hn-title'>(185) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://www.dbreunig.com/2026/04/14/cybersecurity-is-proof-of-work-now.html' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Cybersecurity looks like proof of work now</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>網路安全如今看起來像工作量證明</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47769089' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 529</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47769089' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 197</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--red' data-tag='Security'>Security</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Cybersecurity is increasingly framed as a persistent, resource-intensive effort that mirrors proof-of-work dynamics. Defenders must continually invest to detect, deter, and recover from breaches, creating an ongoing arms race with threat actors. This perspective helps explain why security budgets tend to expand with rising threat capabilities.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>網路防護已成為一場持續且成本高昂的博弈，仿佛工作量證明在起作用。防禦方需要不斷投入資源來偵測、阻擊與修復破口，與威脅方的能力共同推動成本上升。以此觀點看待企業安全預算的成長具有說服力。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://www.dbreunig.com/img/locks_and_keys.jpg' data-full='https://www.dbreunig.com/img/locks_and_keys.jpg' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-47736476' data-tags='AI'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<button class='hn-digest-toggle' type='button' aria-expanded='false' title='Show summary and image' aria-label='Show summary and image'><span aria-hidden='true'>⌄</span></button>
<p class='hn-title'>(186) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://github.com/anthropics/claude-code/issues/46829' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Anthropic downgraded cache TTL on March 6th</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>Anthropic 在3月6日下调 Claude 的缓存 TTL</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47736476' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 524</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47736476' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 400</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--blue' data-tag='AI'>AI</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Anthropic reduced Claude&#x27;s cache TTL on March 6, affecting how fresh responses are served to clients. The change increases cache churn, potentially improving freshness at the cost of latency and API throughput. It signals ongoing tuning of balance between speed, cost, and accuracy.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>3月6日，Anthropic 下调了 Claude 的缓存 TTL，导致返回结果的时效性和新鲜度受到影响。更高的缓存替换率提升了新鲜度，但可能增加延迟与成本，反映出在速度、成本与准确性之间的持续调参。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://opengraph.githubassets.com/a3bb239fbc861bfa12617c1b184dec172d7c035ad6e01e9ca4c2a2d5448cf9f1/anthropics/claude-code/issues/46829' data-full='https://opengraph.githubassets.com/a3bb239fbc861bfa12617c1b184dec172d7c035ad6e01e9ca4c2a2d5448cf9f1/anthropics/claude-code/issues/46829' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-47629433' data-tags='Science,Data'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<button class='hn-digest-toggle' type='button' aria-expanded='false' title='Show summary and image' aria-label='Show summary and image'><span aria-hidden='true'>⌄</span></button>
<p class='hn-title'>(187) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://www.inaturalist.org/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>iNaturalist</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>iNaturalist 自然观测平台</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47629433' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 523</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47629433' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 133</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--teal' data-tag='Science'>Science</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--violet' data-tag='Data'>Data</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>iNaturalist enables crowdsourced biodiversity data from millions of users, driving research and conservation. Its open data catalog helps scientists track species distributions and ecological changes over time. The community-driven platform also highlights the value and challenges of citizen science data.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>iNaturalist 通过海量用户的群体观测，推动生物多样性研究与保护。开放数据目录帮助科学家跟踪物种分布与生态变化，提升研究与教育的可及性。社区驱动的模式同时也暴露了公民科学数据的质量与治理挑战。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://static.inaturalist.org/sites/1-shareable_image.png?1573071871' data-full='https://static.inaturalist.org/sites/1-shareable_image.png?1573071871' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-47890799' data-tags='Career'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<button class='hn-digest-toggle' type='button' aria-expanded='false' title='Show summary and image' aria-label='Show summary and image'><span aria-hidden='true'>⌄</span></button>
<p class='hn-title'>(188) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://kevinlynagh.com/newsletter/2026_04_overthinking/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Sabotaging projects by overthinking, scope creep, and structural diffing</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>过度思考、范围蠕变与结构性差异对项目的破坏</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47890799' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 521</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47890799' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 135</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--sky' data-tag='Career'>Career</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Overthinking, scope creep, and frequent structural diffs create drag on projects, leading to delays and quality issues.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>本文分析过度思考、范围蠕变和频繁的结构性变动如何拖慢项目、造成延迟和质量下降。提供对策：明确范围、记录决策、保持稳定接口、采用小步迭代并快速获取反馈。</p>

<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>The funding round valued the company at $852 billion, signaling strong investor confidence in AI momentum. The result could influence competitors and the broader pace of AI capital and governance considerations.</p>

<p class='hn-title'>(190) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://www.bbc.com/news/videos/ce3d5gkd2geo' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Artemis II crew see first glimpse of far side of Moon [video]</a></p>

<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47649721' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 518</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47649721' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 422</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--teal' data-tag='Space'>Space</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>The Artemis II crew released video glimpses of the Moon&#x27;s far side, a rare up-close look during this crewed lunar test flight. This milestone underscores deep-space operations preparation, relay communications, and the planning groundwork for future crewed lunar missions.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>阿耳忒弥斯二号任务组公布了月球背面的画面片段，这在载人绕月任务中属于难得的近距离观察。该里程碑凸显了深空作业、中继通信以及未来载人登月任务的准备工作。</p>

<div class='hn-body'>

<p class='hn-title'>(191) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://www.science.org/content/article/trump-fires-nsf-s-oversight-board' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Trump fires NSF&#x27;s oversight board</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>特朗普解雇 NSF 监督委员会</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47905283' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 515</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47905283' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 296</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--orange' data-tag='Politics'>Politics</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--teal' data-tag='Science'>Science</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Trump fired the National Science Foundation&#x27;s independent oversight board, a move that reshapes how the agency is governed and funded. Critics warn it reduces external accountability, while supporters say it could speed decision-making on grants and programs.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>特朗普解雇了国家科学基金会的独立监督委员会，此举将重塑机构治理与拨款流程。批评者认为这削弱了外部问责，支持者则称有助于加速资助与项目的决策。</p>
</div>
</div>
</div>

<p class='hn-title'>(192) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://bsky.app/profile/serenityforge.com/post/3mj3r4nbiws2t' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Google removes &quot;Doki Doki Literature Club&quot; from Google Play</a></p>

<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Google removed the game from Play Store, triggering questions about platform moderation. The move highlights how policy decisions affect indie games&#x27; visibility and monetization. It also sparks debate over criteria, consistency, and censorship on major app stores.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>谷歌已将该游戏从应用商店下架，引发关于平台审核与独立游戏曝光之间平衡的讨论。此举凸显了政策决策对独立开发者的可见度与盈利的影响，并引发关于标准、执行一致性以及平台审查尺度的辩论。</p>

<p class='hn-title'>(193) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://www.bbc.com/sport/athletics/articles/crm1m7e0zwzo' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Sawe becomes first athlete to run a sub-two-hour marathon in a competitive race</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>萨韦在正式比赛中实现马拉松2小时内的里程碑</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47914350' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 513</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47914350' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 337</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--teal' data-tag='Science'>Science</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Sawe reportedly becomes the first athlete to run a sub-two-hour marathon in a competitive race, a landmark that presses the boundaries of human endurance. The feat highlights advances in training, pacing, and data-driven preparation enabled by modern sports tech.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>据称萨韦在正式比赛中首次实现马拉松两小时内的里程碑，挑战人类极限。此成就反映出训练、 pacing 与数据驱动的备战在当代体育科技中的进步。</p>

<p class='hn-title'>(194) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://nationaltoday.com/us/tx/austin/news/2026/04/03/oracle-files-thousands-of-h-1b-visa-petitions-amid-mass-layoffs/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Oracle files H-1B visa petitions amid mass layoffs</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>Oracle 在大规模裁员期间提交成千上万份 H-1B 签证申请</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47631732' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 511</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47631732' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 306</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--amber' data-tag='Business'>Business</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--orange' data-tag='Legal'>Legal</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Oracle filed thousands of H-1B visa petitions even as it cut workers, underscoring how tech giants balance layoffs with access to specialized talent. The filings may reflect plans to replace departing staff for critical projects or to maintain a ready pipeline of skilled engineers, drawing attention to immigration policies in the tech industry.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>Oracle 在裁员的同时提交数千份 H-1B 签证申请，凸显巨头在裁员与获取专业人才之间的权衡。相关申请或用于替换离职员工、保障关键项目的人才储备，引发对科技行业移民政策的关注。</p>

<p class='hn-title'>(195) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://worksinprogress.co/issue/why-japan-has-such-good-railways/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Why Japan has such good railways</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>日本铁路为何如此出色</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47815395' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 510</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47815395' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 472</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--rose' data-tag='Culture'>Culture</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--teal' data-tag='Science'>Science</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Explores factors behind Japan&#x27;s top-tier rail network: meticulous maintenance, on-time performance, dense urban planning, and integrated operations. The piece shows how culture, funding, and technology converge to deliver reliable service.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>本文分析日本铁路的四大支撑：精细维护、准点运营、密集的城市规划以及一体化运营。作者指出文化、资金投入与技术协同共同铸就了高水平的铁路服务。</p>

<div class='hn-body'>

<p class='hn-title'>(196) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://twitter.com/__tinygrad__/status/2039213719155310736' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Apple approves driver that lets Nvidia eGPUs work with Arm Macs</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>Apple 批准驱动让 Nvidia eGPU 在 Arm Mac 上工作</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47640380' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 508</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47640380' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 231</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--slate' data-tag='Hardware'>Hardware</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Apple approved a driver enabling Nvidia external GPUs to accelerate Arm-based Macs, expanding hardware options for power users. This marks a notable shift in the Mac ecosystem&#x27;s flexibility.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>苹果批准一款驱动，使 Nvidia 外接显卡在 Arm 架构的 Mac 上工作，扩展了高性能用户的硬件选项。这标志着 Mac 生态在硬件灵活性方面的一次重要转变。</p>

<p class='hn-title'>(197) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://boringbar.app/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Show HN: boringBar – a taskbar-style dock replacement for macOS</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>Show HN：boringBar——macOS 的任务栏风格 Dock 替代</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47742200' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 508</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47742200' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 292</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--green' data-tag='Show HN'>Show HN</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--indigo' data-tag='Programming'>Programming</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>boringBar is a macOS replacement for the Dock with a taskbar-style interface. It emphasizes persistent pinned apps, quick-launch, and consistent shortcuts across spaces, offering an alternative UX to Apple’s native Dock. If it gains traction, it signals demand for more modular, multitasking-oriented macOS UI.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>boringBar 是一个为 macOS 设计的 Dock 替代，采用类似任务栏的界面，便于固定应用、快速启动并在各工作区保持一致。它为多任务工作提供了新的交互方式，若受欢迎，可能反映出对更模块化、以多任务为导向的 macOS UI 的需求。</p>

</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-47721953' data-tags='AI,Programming'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<button class='hn-digest-toggle' type='button' aria-expanded='false' title='Show summary and image' aria-label='Show summary and image'><span aria-hidden='true'>⌄</span></button>
<p class='hn-title'>(198) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://github.com/torvalds/linux/blob/master/Documentation/process/coding-assistants.rst' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>AI assistance when contributing to the Linux kernel</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>AI 辅助 Linux 内核贡献</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47721953' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 505</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47721953' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 388</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--blue' data-tag='AI'>AI</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--indigo' data-tag='Programming'>Programming</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Documentation hints at AI-assisted coding guidance for Linux kernel contributors, illustrating growing acceptance of AI copilots in critical software projects. The move raises questions about tooling, governance, and code quality.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>文档显示 Linux 内核贡献者可以在编码中获得 AI 辅助，反映 AI 助手在关键软件项目中的逐步落地。也带来关于工具治理和代码质量的讨论。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://opengraph.githubassets.com/11e09d3f61dc70f9d25b70e6b1f51480b56abb8ee787fd9c2258f8738bd81fb9/torvalds/linux' data-full='https://opengraph.githubassets.com/11e09d3f61dc70f9d25b70e6b1f51480b56abb8ee787fd9c2258f8738bd81fb9/torvalds/linux' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-47628608' data-tags='Security'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<button class='hn-digest-toggle' type='button' aria-expanded='false' title='Show summary and image' aria-label='Show summary and image'><span aria-hidden='true'>⌄</span></button>
<p class='hn-title'>(199) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://nvd.nist.gov/vuln/detail/CVE-2026-33579' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>OpenClaw privilege escalation vulnerability</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>OpenClaw 提权漏洞（CVE-2026-33579）</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47628608' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 504</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47628608' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 249</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--red' data-tag='Security'>Security</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>A privilege escalation vulnerability in OpenClaw could allow local attackers to obtain higher privileges. Users should patch and update affected installations to mitigate risk.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>OpenClaw 存在本地提权漏洞，潜在攻击者可提升权限。受影响环境需尽快打补丁以降低风险。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://nvd.nist.gov/site-media/images/usbanner/us_flag_small.png' data-full='https://nvd.nist.gov/site-media/images/usbanner/us_flag_small.png' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-47838508' data-tags='Programming,AI'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<button class='hn-digest-toggle' type='button' aria-expanded='false' title='Show summary and image' aria-label='Show summary and image'><span aria-hidden='true'>⌄</span></button>
<p class='hn-title'>(200) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://github.blog/news-insights/company-news/changes-to-github-copilot-individual-plans/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Changes to GitHub Copilot individual plans</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>GitHub Copilot 个人计划调整</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47838508' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 504</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47838508' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 209</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--indigo' data-tag='Programming'>Programming</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--blue' data-tag='AI'>AI</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>GitHub Copilot’s individual plans have changed, with updated pricing and terms affecting solo developers and hobbyists. The move highlights GitHub’s ongoing strategy to monetize AI-assisted coding tools and could shift how individuals adopt the service.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>GitHub Copilot 个人计划发生变动，定价与条款更新，影响单独开发者和爱好者。此举折射出 GitHub 在 AI 助力编码工具上的持续商业化策略，可能改变个人用户的采用路径。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://github.blog/wp-content/uploads/2026/01/generic-github-logo-right.png' data-full='https://github.blog/wp-content/uploads/2026/01/generic-github-logo-right.png' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>
</div>
